*Junpeng Wang,Yuzhong Chen,Menghai Pan,Uday Singh Saini,Yiwei Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLIC，一种通过 token 频率分析与可解释决策树来刻画和比较不同 LLM 编码行为的视觉分析方法，引入鲁棒性与集中度两个新指标，并开发交互式系统支持多尺度探索，从 10 个 LLM、22 个任务的案例研究中提炼模型选择与提示工程建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 编码任务评估主要依赖 pass@k 等性能指标，但随着模型能力提升，许多模型已满足基线要求，性能指标区分度下降；而"不同 LLM 在编码行为上有何差异"这一关键问题尚未得到系统探索。

Method: 提出 CLIC 方法：将每个代码样本表示为 token 频率的特征向量，训练可解释的决策树以区分两个 LLM 的代码集；除分类准确率外，定义鲁棒性（逐步移除最具判别力 token 后两个 LLM 是否仍可区分）与集中度（差异由少数主导 token 驱动还是分散在众多 token 上）两个新指标；并构建交互式视觉分析系统，支持在 LLM 对、任务和分词层级等多尺度上导航比较空间、定位感兴趣的配对并下钻到判别性 token 及其代码上下文。

Result: 通过对 10 个 LLM 在 22 个 Kaggle 机器学习任务上的成对比较案例研究，发现了关于 LLM 选择与提示工程的可操作洞见。

Conclusion: CLIC 超越了单一性能指标，从编码行为角度揭示 LLM 之间的差异，为模型选择与提示工程提供了新视角与实用指导，弥补了现有评估方法的不足。

Abstract: The evaluation of large language models (LLMs) on coding tasks has primarily focused on performance metrics such as pass@k. As LLMs continue to advance, many models now meet baseline performance requirements, reducing the discriminative power of performance-based evaluation alone. Yet a key question remains largely unexplored: how do LLMs differ in their coding behavior? We propose CLIC (Code Learning for Identification and Comparison), a visual analytics approach that characterizes LLM coding behavior through token-frequency analysis. CLIC represents each code sample as a feature vector of token frequencies and trains an interpretable decision tree to separate two LLMs' code sets. Beyond classification accuracy, we define two new metrics: robustness, which measures whether the two LLMs remain distinguishable as their most-discriminative tokens are progressively removed, and concentration, which measures whether the difference is driven by a few dominant tokens or spread across many. Interpreting numerous pairwise comparisons (across LLM pairs, tasks, and tokenization levels) and tracing the full analytical chain form an inherently multi-scale, hypothesis-driven exploration task. We therefore develop an interactive visual analytics system to navigate the comparison landscape, identify pairs of interest, and drill down into discriminative tokens and their code contexts. Case studies comparing 10 LLMs across 22 Kaggle ML tasks reveal actionable insights for LLM selection and prompt engineering.

</details>


### [58] [TreeSpark: Calibrated, Load-Adaptive Draft Trees for Semi-Autoregressive Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22098)
*Huapeng Zhou,Huayu Wang,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TreeSpark 是一种基于父条件分布的树形推测解码方法，通过边缘接受度校准与路径生存驱动的动态扩展，在无温度与任意温度下无损加速大语言模型推理，相比固定预算树和链式草稿接受更多 token 并提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块草稿器使草稿生成几乎零成本，但树形草稿的候选排序依赖逐位置边际分布，忽略候选的父节点，导致半自回归草稿器上宽树主要增加错误排序的节点；同时固定大小的树无法适配每轮可承受的推测量与不同服务负载。

Method: TreeSpark 从草稿器已有的马尔可夫头以可忽略成本读取父条件分布，将其校准为边缘接受度估计，并以路径存活为核心驱动最佳优先扩展、逐轮停止和负载自适应服务策略；通过无放回采样兄弟节点并在递归拒绝中匹配残差，保证任意温度下解码无损。

Result: 自适应树在任意温度下均优于匹配的固定预算树；相对于同草稿器上经过调优的链式方法，TreeSpark 每轮多接受 15-25% 的草稿 token，单请求墙钟时间解码提速 8-14%，并在负载上升时平滑收缩树结构回到链式。

Conclusion: TreeSpark 证明利用父条件分布校准与路径生存驱动的动态树扩展，能在保持无损性的同时有效提升块草稿器下推测解码的效率和负载适应性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates language-model inference by letting a cheap drafter propose tokens that the target model verifies in parallel. Recent block drafters make drafting nearly free: a single backbone pass emits an entire block of draft tokens. Draft trees promise a further gain -- several alternative continuations verified in one target forward -- but existing constructions rank candidates by per-position marginals that ignore which parent a candidate extends, so on semi-autoregressive drafters wider trees mostly add mis-ranked nodes; and a tree of fixed size ignores how much speculation each decoding round, and each serving load, can support. We introduce TreeSpark, which reads a parent-conditioned distribution from the drafter's existing Markov head at negligible cost, calibrates it into an edge-acceptance estimate, and lets path survival govern everything else: best-first expansion, per-round stopping, and a load-adaptive serving policy. Sampling siblings without replacement, with matching residuals in recursive rejection, keeps decoding lossless at any temperature. Adaptive trees improve on matched fixed budgets at every temperature; against a tuned chain on the same drafter, TreeSpark accepts 15-25% more draft tokens per round and decodes 8-14% faster in single-request wall-clock, and under rising load it gracefully shrinks the tree back to the chain. Code and artifacts: https://github.com/PopSoda2002/TreeSpark

</details>


### [59] [A framework for recipe data structure with applications for culinary and nutritional insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22099)
*Mansi Goel,Sumit Bhagat,Saloni Srivastava,Malav Patel,Shlok Vinodkumar Mehroliya,Ganesh Bagler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 RecipeDB2，一个将 128,942 份食谱结构化为可查询数据的框架：通过基于 transformer 的命名实体模型解析配料短语，用 BERT 嵌入将配料链接到 USDA 营养数据库，再用随机森林分类饮食类别并赋予地域文化与膳食标签，使食谱从自由文本转化为可计算的营养与地理文化分析对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食谱主要以自由文本形式存在，人可读但机器不可直接计算；现有食谱数据集仅捕获零散信息，缺少一个能在单一可查询模式中同时连接食谱的结构化配料组成、地理文化来源与营养谱系的统一表示。

Method: 作者形式化了一套食谱数据结构框架，将每份食谱分解为带类型的配料实体，并依托参考营养数据库进行实体链接。具体实现包括：用基于 transformer 的命名实体模型将配料短语解析为七种烹饪属性；通过 BERT 嵌入策略将配料链接到 USDA 参考表（在 200 个最高频配料的人工裁定集上 F1=87.90），为每个映射配料生成 148 项营养参数；用随机森林分类器在全量词汇上传播 34 个配料类别；并用确定性的保守规则集为每份食谱分配一种膳食风格。

Result: 构建出 RecipeDB2，收录 128,942 份食谱、35,474 种配料，覆盖 32 个地区与 99 个国家；配料链接取得 F1=87.90，每份映射配料获得 148 项营养参数，并完成了 34 类配料分类与食谱膳食风格标注。

Conclusion: RecipeDB2 论证了一种可扩展的框架，使食谱变得可计算，将长期以来被视为艺术而非定量对象的烹饪文化遗产转化为可供数据驱动分析的研究资源。

Abstract: Cooking is a complex process that transforms raw ingredients into delicious and nutritious dishes, yet the recipes that encode this process remain largely free text; readable by people but not directly computable. Existing recipe collections capture fragments of this information, but no shared representation links a recipe's structured ingredient composition, its geo-cultural provenance, and its nutritional profile within a single queryable schema. We address this representation gap by formalizing a framework for recipe data structure that decomposes each recipe into typed ingredient entities, grounds those entities in a reference nutritional database, and annotates them with geo-cultural and dietary context. We present RecipeDB2, a structured compilation of 128,942 recipes with 35,474 ingredients from 32 regions and 99 countries. Ingredient phrases are parsed into seven culinary attributes using a transformer-based named-entity model; ingredients are linked to the USDA reference tables through a BERT embedding strategy (F1 = 87.90 on a manually adjudicated set of the 200 most frequent ingredients), yielding 148 nutritional parameters per mapped ingredient; a Random Forest classifier propagates 34 ingredient categories across the full vocabulary; and a deterministic, conservative rule set assigns each recipe a dietary style. Through RecipeDB2 (https://cosylab.iiitd.edu.in/recipedb2/), we demonstrate a scalable framework for making recipes computable, turning culinary heritage (long treated as an artistic rather than a quantitative object) into a data-driven analysis.

</details>


### [60] [AdaMem: Adaptive Memory Token Allocation for Soft Compression in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22100)
*Artem Sakhno,Grigorii Davydenko,Omar Zoloev,Julia Belikova,Andrey Savchenko,Maksim Makarenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AdaMem 提出一种相关性引导的软压缩方法，在固定记忆预算下按段落与查询的相关性动态分配连续记忆嵌入，相比均匀分配基线（OSCAR）在多个开放域 QA 基准上显著提升答案质量并降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 处理大量长段落成本高且会引入干扰信息，软压缩虽能将段落编码为紧凑的记忆嵌入，但现有方法对不同相关性的段落分配相同数量的记忆嵌入，忽略了查询特定的相关性差异，导致在记忆预算有限时资源分配不合理。

Method: 提出 AdaMem：一个共享的查询条件压缩器在单次前向中同时产生连续段落记忆和相关分数；学到的段落相关性估计映射为固定记忆 token 预算的查询相关分配，通过确定性分配规则给高分段落更多记忆 token，并可省略低分段落。

Result: 在六个开放域 QA 基准上，AdaMem 在匹配预算下一致优于 OSCAR 及其他软压缩方法。16× 压缩下子串匹配较均匀分配基线最高提升 3.2 点（5.5%），平均相对增益 3.4%；64× 压缩下平均相对增益达 14.6%，PopQA 上最大提升 9.8 点（19.7%）。AdaMem 在答案质量与无压缩相当时，推理延迟比全上下文基线低至 4 倍。

Conclusion: 相关性引导的记忆分配在检索池大、可用记忆预算紧张时尤为有效，AdaMem 在保持与均匀压缩基线相当的效率的同时，显著提升了软压缩 RAG 的性能。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language models with retrieved evidence, but processing many long passages is costly and can introduce distracting information. Soft compression addresses this challenge by encoding passages as compact sequences of continuous memory embeddings before generation. However, existing methods typically assign each retained passage an identical number of memory embeddings, irrespective of its query-specific relevance. To address this, we propose AdaMem, a relevance-guided soft-compression framework that maps learned passage-relevance estimates to a query-dependent allocation of a fixed memory-token budget. A shared query-conditioned compressor produces both continuous passage memories and relevance scores in a single pass; a deterministic allocation rule assigns more memory tokens to higher-scoring passages and can omit low-scoring ones. Across six open-domain QA benchmarks, AdaMem consistently outperforms OSCAR (the closely matched soft-compression baseline that uses uniform allocation) as well as other soft-compression methods at matched memory budgets. Under standard 16$\times$ compression, AdaMem improves sub-string match by up to 3.2 points (5.5%) over uniform allocation baseline, with an average relative gain of 3.4%; under aggressive 64$\times$ compression the average relative gain grows to 14.6%, with a maximum of 9.8 points (19.7%) on PopQA. AdaMem matches the answer quality of the uncompressed at up to 4$\times$ lower inference latency than full context baseline. AdaMem retains an efficiency profile comparable to the uniform-compression baseline, while achieving up to $4\times$ lower inference latency than full-context inference. Thus, relevance-guided memory allocation is particularly effective when retrieval pools are large and the available memory budget is tight.

</details>


### [61] [Context Poisoning as Extreme-Value Attention Interference in Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22101)
*Meysam Ghaffari,Nina Fatehi,Bhaskar Sen,Nasim Sabetpour,Carlos Morato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"上下文污染"概念，将其建模为注意力中的极值干扰，并推导出有限样本上界，证明在softmax检索抽象下维持固定准确率需要证据边际按Ω(√log N)增长；实验表明同格式构造的干扰项导致最大下降，检索门控可改善证据利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽能处理越来越长的提示，但随着无关或易混淆上下文增加，其定位和使用决定性证据的能力会退化。现有研究多关注原始上下文长度，而未从注意力极值干扰角度系统刻画并量化这一退化机制及其所需的证据边际增长条件。

Method: 将上下文污染建模为注意力中的极值干扰：决定性证据得分有上界，而有效干扰项的最大得分随其数量增长。在softmax检索抽象下，推导有限样本上界，并证明维持固定准确率目标需证据边际按Ω(√log N)增长，其中N为有效干扰项数量而非原始上下文长度。分析将长上下文退化与得分混叠、位置混叠及softmax稀释联系起来。通过控制实验，在嵌入难负样本的条件下考察总上下文增长、同格式干扰构造、以及检索门控对检索准确率和证据利用的影响。

Result: 理论推导显示维持固定准确率需证据边际按Ω(√log N)增长。控制实验表明：在存在嵌入难负样本时，检索准确率随总上下文增长而下降；在固定上下文长度下，同格式条件在所有测试的干扰构造中产生最大准确率降幅；检索门控可改善证据利用，但其净收益取决于能否保持证据召回率。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持证据瓶颈、抗混叠表示、先检索后推理架构、验证者中介记忆及对比式抗污染训练等方向，为缓解长上下文下的上下文污染问题提供了理论与实验依据。

Abstract: Large language models can process increasingly long prompts, yet their ability to locate and use decisive evidence may degrade as irrelevant or confusable context is added. We formulate this phenomenon, which we call context poisoning, as extreme-value interference in attention: the decisive-evidence score is upper-bounded, while the maximum score among effective distractors grows with their number. Under a softmax retrieval abstraction, we derive a finite-sample upper bound showing that maintaining a fixed accuracy target above base rate requires the evidence margin to scale as $Ω(\sqrt{\log N})$, where N denotes the effective distractor count rather than necessarily the raw context length. The analysis connects long-context degradation to score aliasing, positional aliasing, and softmax dilution. Controlled experiments show that retrieval accuracy decreases as total context grows in the presence of embedded hard negatives, that the same-format condition produces the largest observed accuracy drop among the tested distractor constructions at fixed context length, and that retrieval gating can improve evidence use while its net benefit depends on preserving evidence recall. These results motivate evidence bottlenecks, alias-resistant representations, retrieve-then-reason architectures, verifier-mediated memory, and contrastive anti-poison training.

</details>


### [62] [DeepInstructor: An Agentic AI Instructor for Experience-Driven Idea Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22104)
*Rongcan Pei,Fang Guo,Qinglin Qi,Qi Zhu,Yun Luo,Jianhao Yan,Minjun Zhu,Qiujie Xie,Dehong Zheng,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DeepInstructor 提出一个基于智能体（agentic）框架，将科研想法评估形式化为对结构化学术经验的推理：它从 58,607 篇同行评审构建“经验图谱”，并用 ReAct 智能体检索维度特定证据，实现可追溯评估；配套数据集 DeepInstruct 在新颖性、重要性、可行性上进行受控成对比较，评估对齐人类判断的 Hit@1/Hit@2 分别提升 24.4% 和 29.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 推动自动化科研发现，想法生成瓶颈转移到想法评估；现有评估器主要依赖参数化 LLM 知识或非结构化检索，缺乏人类导师基于经验的推理过程。

Method: 提出 DeepInstructor：从 58,607 篇同行评审构建 Experience Graph（经验图谱），采用 ReAct 智能体检索维度特定证据，使评估可追溯；并构建 DeepInstruct 数据集，包含新颖性、重要性、可行性三个维度的受控成对比较。

Result: DeepInstructor 显著优于现有基线，与人类判断对齐的 Hit@1 提升 24.4%，Hit@2 提升 29.7%。

Conclusion: 科研想法评估可以通过对结构化学术经验的显式推理来实现（而非仅依赖参数化知识或非结构化检索），为可追溯、经验驱动的自动评估提供了新方向。

Abstract: As automated scientific discovery advances, Large Language Models (LLMs) can now generate research ideas at an unprecedented scale, shifting the bottleneck from idea generation to idea evaluation. Existing evaluators mainly rely on parametric LLM knowledge or unstructured retrieval, producing judgments that lack the experience-grounded reasoning used by human instructors. To address this, we propose DeepInstructor, an agentic framework that formulates idea evaluation as reasoning over structured scholarly experience. DeepInstructor constructs an Experience Graph from 58,607 peer reviews and employs a ReAct-based agent to retrieve dimension-specific evidence for traceable evaluation. We further introduce DeepInstruct, a dataset with controlled pairwise comparisons across novelty, significance, and feasibility. Experiments show that DeepInstructor substantially outperforms existing baselines, improving Hit@1 and Hit@2 alignment with human judgments by 24.4% and 29.7%, respectively. Our findings suggest that scientific idea evaluation can be grounded in explicit reasoning over structured scholarly experience

</details>


### [63] [Evaluating Fine-Tuned and Base Language Models in Maternal and Vaccination Healthcare for African Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22110)
*Abdulquddus Ajibade,Oluwaseun Odunsi,Iyinoluwa Animasaun,Chioma Nwakanma-Akanno,Oluwasegun Oguntuase,Oluwafunke Akinbuwa,Abiodun Adereni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究对比了针对尼日利亚母婴健康与疫苗接种领域微调的MamaBot-Llama和Vax-Llama与基座模型Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct的表现，发现母婴模型显著提升，而疫苗模型因数据质量欠佳反而显著退化，强调领域微调需高质量数据与严格验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）有望改善低资源环境下的医疗信息传递，但可能生成不准确或文化上不恰当的医疗建议，因此需要评估针对特定领域微调能否提升其在尼日利亚母婴健康与疫苗接种场景中的可靠性。

Method: 使用LoRA对Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct进行微调：MamaBot-Llama基于超过36,000条母婴健康问答对，Vax-Llama基于9,000余条疫苗接种问答对。评估采用200个医疗问题（母婴健康与疫苗接种各100个，各覆盖5个子领域），由两名尼日利亚执业医师按5点Likert量表独立评分，涵盖准确性、安全性、清晰度、情境适当性与可信度五个维度，并用Wilcoxon符号秩检验进行配对比较。

Result: MamaBot-Llama在所有指标上均显著优于基座模型，总体提升4.9%（p < .001），其中临床可信度提升7%、医疗准确性提升5%，严重问题减少50%，78%的案例中临床医生更青睐该模型。相反，Vax-Llama总体下降5.2%（p < .001），严重问题增加192%，安全问题增加400%。

Conclusion: 基于高质量、临床医师整理的领域特定微调能够提升医疗LLM的表现，但当微调数据集质量不足时反而会降低性能。在临床部署前必须进行严格的领域特定验证，同时研究遵循了知情同意与聊天日志匿名化等伦理规范。

Abstract: Background: Large language models (LLMs) can improve healthcare information delivery in low-resource settings but may produce inaccurate or culturally inappropriate advice. This study evaluated domain-specific fine-tuning for maternal health and vaccination in Nigeria. Objective: To compare HelpMum's MamaBot-Llama and Vax-Llama with Meta's Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct for accuracy, safety, clarity, contextual appropriateness, and trustworthiness. Methods: We evaluated 200 healthcare questions, 100 each for maternal health and vaccination, across five subdomains per domain. MamaBot-Llama and Vax-Llama were fine-tuned using Low-Rank Adaptation on over 36,000 maternal health and 9,000 vaccination question-answer pairs, respectively. Two Nigerian licensed physicians independently rated responses using a 5-point Likert scale. Paired comparisons used Wilcoxon signed-rank tests. Results: Performance varied by domain. MamaBot-Llama significantly outperformed the base model across all criteria, with a 4.9% overall improvement (p < .001), including gains in clinical trustworthiness (+7%) and medical accuracy (+5%). Critical issues decreased by 50%, and clinicians preferred it in 78% of cases. In contrast, Vax-Llama showed a 5.2% overall decline (p < .001), with critical issues increasing by 192% and safety concerns by 400%. Conclusions: Domain-specific fine-tuning can improve healthcare LLM performance when based on high-quality, clinician-curated data, but may also degrade performance when dataset quality is inadequate. Rigorous domain-specific validation is essential before clinical deployment. Physician evaluators provided informed consent, and chatbot logs were anonymized. Keywords: Large language models; Fine-tuning; Maternal health; Vaccination; Healthcare AI; Low-resource settings; Nigeria; Model evaluation; LoRA; Medical accuracy

</details>


### [64] [Beyond the Text: Verifying That Agent-Written Papers Are Backed by Their Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22111)
*Qiuhong Shen,Benlong Wu,Hanjin Liu,Yuang Qi,Kejiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReAgent 是一个自动化审计框架，通过静态分析与动态执行证据相结合，评估智能体生成的研究文档与其代码仓库之间的一致性，以发现硬编码指标、未实现方法、不支持实验结果等不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体已能自主开展研究并生成文档与代码，但现有评审主要评估文本质量，无法可靠识别文档声明与仓库实现/执行证据之间的不一致（如硬编码指标、方法未实现、实验结果缺乏支撑），因此需要一种面向"文档-仓库"一致性的自动化审计方法。

Method: ReAgent 从研究文档中构建科学声明的结构化表示，并以此指导仓库分析与证据收集。静态审计检查所声明的方法、实现与实验配置是否与仓库一致；动态审计执行相关实验并采集执行证据以评估经验发现。两者结合可发现单一视角下隐藏的不一致（如复现数字但偏离声明方法）。最终将证据与审计结论整理为结构化的仓库级审计报告，保障证据可追溯。

Result: 在人工整理的智能体生成"研究文档-仓库"对基准上，与代表性静态基线和基于复现的基线相比，ReAgent 能有效识别报告研究发现与其支撑性仓库证据之间的不一致。

Conclusion: ReAgent 证明了将静态分析与动态执行证据相结合能够有效审计智能体生成研究的一致性，为标识文档声明与仓库实现/实验结果之间的不一致提供了可追溯的自动化方案。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly capable of conducting research autonomously, producing research documents alongside the code and experiments that ostensibly support them. Yet whether the reported findings are consistently supported by corresponding implementations and execution evidence remains largely unexplored: existing review practices primarily assess textual quality and cannot reliably identify inconsistencies such as hard-coded metrics, unimplemented methods, or unsupported experimental results. We present ReAgent, an automated auditing framework for assessing the consistency between agent-generated research documents and their associated repositories. ReAgent constructs structured representations of scientific claims from research documents and uses them to guide repository analysis and evidence collection. Static auditing examines whether claimed methodologies, implementations, and experimental configurations are consistently reflected in the repository, while dynamic auditing executes relevant experiments and collects execution evidence to assess empirical findings. By combining static analysis with dynamic evidence, ReAgent identifies inconsistencies that may remain hidden under either perspective alone, such as experiments that reproduce reported numbers while deviating from the claimed methodology. The collected evidence and audit decisions are organized into a structured repository-level audit report, enabling transparent evidence traceability. We evaluate ReAgent on a manually curated benchmark of agent-generated research document--repository pairs and compare it against representative static and reproduction-based baselines. Experimental results demonstrate that ReAgent effectively identifies inconsistencies between reported research findings and their supporting repository evidence.

</details>


### [65] [Privacy Personalization Trade offs in LLMs: The Impact of Stylometric Signal Reduction on User-Specific Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22112)
*Muhammed Nazmul Arefin,Omar Jamal Hammad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型个性化文本生成中的隐私-个性化权衡：通过LaMP-7 Twitter基准对250名用户进行受控实验，发现去除可识别文体信号的匿名化条件使模型输出的偏好度骤降至13.0%，而语义保留仍高达94.8%，揭示出保留语义与抑制文体指纹之间存在的根本性矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽然能高保真地生成用户个性化文本，但支撑这种个性化的个人数据常嵌含人口统计、文化及文体标记，引发文体计量学再识别（stylometric re-identification）的隐私担忧。作者希望探究：削弱可识别文体信号是否会损害LLM文本生成的个性化效果，即隐私与个性化之间是否存在权衡。

Method: 作者提出一个受控框架，在LaMP-7 Twitter基准上隔离文体信号。实验抽取250名用户，设置两种改写条件：一是以原始画像为条件的改写；二是以匿名化转换画像为条件的改写（系统性地中和人口统计标识、文化引用、个人细节与非正式语言线索）。输出由两个独立的LLM评判者及补充的人工评估共同评测，采用成对比较评估方式。

Result: 成对评估显示，以原始画像为条件的输出与人工撰写的真实文本几乎无法区分，表明现代LLM能以足够保真度复现作者写作风格；而以匿名化画像为条件的模型输出偏好度平均降至13.0%，但语义上下文保留仍高达94.8%。人工评估者研究确认了相同的模式。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了明确的隐私-个性化权衡，突显了开发隐私感知型个性化方法的需求，即在保留语义的同时抑制可识别的文体信号。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated the ability to generate user-specific text with high stylistic fidelity. However, the personal data that enables such personalization frequently embeds demographic, cultural, and stylistic markers that raises concerns about stylometric re- identification. This paper investigates whether reducing identifiable stylistic signals affects personalization in text generation by LLMs. We introduce a controlled framework to isolate stylometric signals in LLM personalization using the LaMP-7 Twitter benchmark. Experiments on 250 sampled users compare two settings: paraphrasing conditioned on the original profile and paraphrasing conditioned on an anonymized converted profile in which demographic identifiers, cultural references, personal details, and informal linguistic cues have been systematically neutralized. Outputs are assessed by two independent LLM judges and a complementary human evaluation. Our pairwise evaluation shows that outputs conditioned on original profiles are nearly indistinguishable from human-authored ground truth, indicating that modern LLMs can closely reproduce an author's writing style with sufficient fidelity. In contrast, preference for model outputs with anonymized profiles drops to 13.0% on average, while semantic context preservation remains high at 94.8%. A study with human evaluators confirms the same pattern. These findings reveal a clear privacy-personalization trade-off and highlight the need for privacy-aware personalization methods that retain meaning while suppressing identifying stylistic signals.

</details>


### [66] [An Empirical Cost Attribution of Context-Compression Gateways in Multi-Turn Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22114)
*Luzhuo Chen,Jiayu Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过在生产压缩网关（Paritok）上对真实多轮编码代理会话进行A/B测试，将token账单分解为三个独立杠杆（工具schema过滤、内容压缩、历史摘要），证明三者节省速率根本不同：工具schema过滤是唯一稳定为正的杠杆，内容压缩单轮仅省约2%但因历史累积重发呈二次方增长并受上下文窗口封顶，且召回重发会逐段消耗节省；单轮压缩基准的高分与多轮成本正交，不能作为省钱论据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文压缩被广泛用于降低LLM编码代理的token开销，公开基准显示激进压缩能保持任务求解质量，但这两个事实并不能推导出被普遍默认的第三个事实——即在真实多轮代理中压缩文件读取确实能省钱。缺少对真实生产会话token账单的分解与量化测量。

Method: 在编码代理（Claude Code、Codex）与前沿LLM（Claude Sonnet、GPT-5）之间部署生产压缩网关Paritok，将真实会话的token账单分解为三个独立杠杆：工具schema过滤、文件读取内容与工具输出的压缩、历史摘要。对每一杠杆在受控A/B实验中单独测量其节省量，并分析其随轮次N的增长规律（线性、二次方、受上下文窗口封顶）以及非破坏性召回（recall）的成本边界。同时对比部署模型Paritok-4B在SWE-bench上25.7%压缩率下保留86.5%质量这一单轮基准结果。

Result: 工具schema过滤每轮移除固定块（典型轮约21K-57K tokens），随轮次N线性增长，是唯一明确且可复现为正的杠杆。内容压缩单轮仅节省约2%的缓存定价前缀，但压缩后的读取会累积进历史并在后续每轮重发，累计节省约为3350*N^2 tokens（实测），约6轮内即超过固定的工具过滤节省，直到被上下文窗口封顶。非破坏性网关允许代理按需取回原始字节，每次召回恰好重发刚被压缩掉的那一段，成本固定且有界（而非乘法式膨胀），重度召回会逐段消耗掉累积的节省。单轮压缩基准（25.7%压缩率下保留86.5% SWE-bench质量）与多轮代理成本正交，不能作为省钱依据。

Conclusion: 三种节省杠杆速率根本不同且不可混同：工具schema过滤是唯一稳定可靠的正杠杆；内容压缩的收益呈二次增长但受上下文窗口限制，且被按需召回逐段抵消；单轮压缩质量基准与多轮成本正交。据此提炼出可操作的配方，指明token节省应在何处投入才真正有回报。

Abstract: Context compression is widely proposed as a way to cut the token bill of LLM coding agents, and public benchmarks report that aggressive compression preserves task-solving quality. These two facts do not imply the third one commonly assumed: that compressing file reads saves money in a real multi-turn agent. We instrument a production compression gateway (Paritok) between coding agents (Claude Code, Codex) and frontier LLMs (Claude Sonnet, GPT-5), and decompose the token bill of real sessions into three independent levers: tool-schema filtering, content compression of file reads and tool output, and history summarization. Measured in isolation under controlled A/B runs, the three save at fundamentally different rates. Tool-schema filtering removes a fixed block every turn, roughly 21K-57K tokens on a typical turn; it is linear in the turn count N and the only unambiguously and reproducibly positive lever. Content compression saves only about 2% of the cache-priced prefix per turn, but compressed reads accumulate in history and are re-sent on every later turn, so its cumulative saving grows quadratically, about 3350*N^2 tokens (measured), overtaking the fixed tool-filter saving within roughly 6 turns until the context window caps it. A non-destructive gateway lets the agent pull original bytes back on demand; each recall re-sends exactly the one segment just compressed away, so its cost is fixed and bounded rather than a multiplicative blowup, and heavy recall spends the accumulated saving back one segment at a time. Finally, a strong single-shot compression benchmark - 86.5% of SWE-bench quality retained at a 25.7% compression rate, achieved by the model this gateway deploys (Paritok-4B, reported separately) - is orthogonal to multi-turn agent cost and must not be cited as a cost-saving argument. We distill the results into an actionable recipe for where token-saving effort pays off.

</details>


### [67] [Evaluation Awareness Shifts from Format to Context with Model Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22119)
*Navraj Singh,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探究模型如何检测“评估意识”，并发现小型模型依赖提示格式敏感性、大型模型依赖高阶推理；据此提出“提示清洗+激活反引导”的双通路干预，在200个高评估意识提示上实现70.58%的平均行为翻转率，说明抑制评估意识需同时处理提示层和表征层信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估意识对模型评估构成前所未有的威胁，但模型检测评估意识的机制尚不明确；研究旨在揭示机制并区分小模型与大模型的差异，从而设计更有效的抑制手段。

Method: 使用链式思维分析、表征探测和积分梯度归因三类方法，评估 Gemma 3（1B/4B/12B）、Phi-3（Mini/Medium）与 Llama-3 8B；并基于机制发现提出双通路干预，结合提示清洗（去除外部评估触发信号）与激活反引导（抑制内部表征信号），在200个高评估意识提示上进行验证。

Result: 小模型通过提示的格式敏感性检测评估，大模型多依赖高阶推理；双通路干预在200个高评估意识提示上取得70.58%的平均行为翻转率，一致优于任一单独干预。

Conclusion: 紧凑型语言模型的评估意识随规模增长而发展，其形成机制在不同规模间不同；有效缓解须联合处理提示级与表征级信号，为研究模型如何“感知被评估”及制定稳健评估策略提供了新见解。

Abstract: Evaluation awareness poses an unprecedented threat to model evaluation, but the mechanisms by which models detect it remain unknown. This study focuses on determining this and identifying contrasting mechanisms between smaller and larger models. While smaller models use the prompt's format sensitivity to detect evaluation, larger models often rely on higher-order reasoning to detect it. We evaluated Gemma 3 (1B, 4B, and 12B), Phi-3 (Mini and Medium), and Llama-3 8B using Chain-of-Thought analysis, representation probing, and Integrated Gradients attribution. Motivated by these findings, we propose a dual-pathway intervention that combines prompt sanitization with activation counter-steering to suppress both external evaluation triggers and their internal representations. Across 200 highly evaluation-aware prompts, our method achieves an average behavioral flip rate of 70.58\%, consistently outperforming either intervention alone. These results provide new insights into how evaluation awareness develops in compact language models and suggest that effective mitigation requires jointly addressing both prompt-level and representation-level signals.Datasets and codebase can be found in this \href{https://github.com/chahal-navi/Evaluation-Awareness-Compact-LLMs/tree/main}{Github Repository.}

</details>


### [68] [Balancing Reasoning and Hardware Constraints in RAG Pipelines for Ukrainian Multi-Domain Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22124)
*Illya Havrylov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了提交到 UNLP 2026 多域文档理解共享任务的系统：在 9 小时离线 Kaggle 限制下，针对乌克兰语 PDF 语料提取答案、文档 ID 和页码。由于扫描文档 OCR 耗时 5-7 小时，剩余 LLM 推理时间极紧，团队采用混合 RAG 流水线（BM25、BGE-M3、交叉编码器重排）配合 4-bit 量化 LapaLLM 12B 模型，最终私有分数 0.8095，排名第 10/15。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享任务的严格 9 小时离线执行限制使 OCR 成为主要瓶颈（单线程串行执行耗时 5-7 小时），仅剩约 2 小时用于约 500 个问题的 LLM 推理。参数庞大的推理模型（如 DeepSeek R1）持续超时，因此必须设计资源高效且能保证不超时的流水线。

Method: 采用资源高效的混合检索增强生成（RAG）流水线：使用 BM25、BGE-M3 和 Cross-Encoder 重排进行检索；推理端放弃大参数推理模型，改用 4-bit 量化的 LapaLLM 12B 模型，通过 llama.cpp 部署在双 NVIDIA T4 GPU 上，优先保障流水线稳定性而非多步推理。

Result: 系统在隐藏私有测试集上取得 Private Score 0.8095，在 15 支活跃参赛队伍中排名第 10。通过避免超时并控制 OCR 与推理开销，成功在时间预算内完成全部处理。

Conclusion: 在严格时间约束下，权衡模型能力与资源效率、优先保障流水线稳定性是可行的策略；混合 RAG 加量化小模型的方案能够在保证按时完成的前提下取得具有竞争力的成绩，而大参数推理模型因超时并不适用。

Abstract: This paper describes the system submitted to the UNLP 2026 Shared Task on Multi-Domain Document Understanding. The challenge required extracting precise answers, document IDs, and page numbers from a diverse corpus of Ukrainian PDF documents within a strict 9-hour offline Kaggle execution limit. During evaluation on the hidden private test set, optical character recognition (OCR) of scanned documents emerged as a severe bottleneck, consuming 5-7 hours of the total time budget due to sequential single-threaded execution. This overhead strictly limited the remaining time for Large Language Model (LLM) inference to approximately two hours for 500 questions. To guarantee pipeline completion without timeouts, we developed a resource-efficient Hybrid Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline utilizing BM25, BGE-M3, and Cross-Encoder reranking. Rather than deploying parameter-heavy reasoning models (e.g., DeepSeek R1) which consistently timed out, we utilized a 4-bit quantized LapaLLM 12B model via llama.cpp on dual NVIDIA T4 GPUs. Prioritizing pipeline stability over multi-step reasoning, our system achieved a Private Score of 0.8095, placing 10th out of 15 active teams.

</details>


### [69] [Type-Driven Tokenization for Brahmic Scripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22125)
*Sai Hemanth Kapila,Rakshika Bagavathy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出标准分词器在处理婆罗米系文字（天城文、泰卢固文、泰米尔文、卡纳达文等元音附标文字）时会产生非法文本，根源在于这些文字的正字法不满足自由拼接性质，并用形式化方法（Agda）证明并提出一个可修复该问题的工具与补丁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型使用的标准分词器在处理婆罗米系文字时会产生畸形文本。婆罗米系文字是元音附标文字，其辅音带有固有元音，且可由附加符号修饰，因而存在字母文字（如英文）所没有的正字法约束。作者观察到英文正字法构成一个“半群”（任意两个合法词元可自由拼接），而婆罗米文正字法只构成“部分半群”（并非所有拼接都产生合法字符串）。这一区别是分词器产生错误输出的根本原因。

Method: 作者在 Agda 中将“半群”与“部分半群”的区分加以形式化；将合法的婆罗米词元建模为迁移系统中的链；并由此推导出可证明正确的 fixToken 函数，该函数能将任意候选词元扩展为符合正字法边界的词元。随后将该形式化推导转化为两套实用工具：针对 SentencePiece 的实际补丁，以及一个独立的基于 Rust 的预分词器库。

Result: 该方法成功消除了各印度系文字中观察到的分词错误：SentencePiece 补丁和独立的 Rust 预分词器库均能产出符合婆罗米正字法约束的合法词元，且 fixToken 的正确性在 Agda 中得到形式化证明。

Conclusion: 婆罗米系文字分词错误的根源在于其正字法仅构成部分半群而非半群。通过形式化建模并推导出正确性可证的 fixToken 函数，可将其转化为实用的 SentencePiece 补丁与 Rust 预分词器库，从而在多种印度系文字中彻底消除分词错误，为处理非字母文字系统提供了形式化驱动的解决方案。

Abstract: Standard tokenizers used in large language models produce malformed text when applied to Brahmic scripts. They are a family of abugidas, writing systems whose consonants carry an inherent vowel that dependent marks can modify. They include Devanagari, Telugu, Tamil, Kannada, and others. The underlying issue is that these tokenizers violate orthographic constraints that do not arise in alphabetic scripts like English. We observe that while English orthography forms a \emph{semigroup} (any two valid tokens can be freely concatenated), Brahmic orthography forms a \emph{partial semigroup}: not every concatenation yields a valid string. We formalise this distinction in Agda, model valid Brahmic tokens as chains in a transition system, and derive a provably correct \texttt{fixToken} function that extends any candidate token to respect orthographic boundaries. We then show how this formal derivation translates into a practical patch for SentencePiece as well as a standalone Rust-based pre-tokenizer library, eliminating the observed errors across Indic scripts.

</details>


### [70] [Beyond Accuracy and Surface Fluency: Risk-Sensitive Evaluation of LLMs for Legal Clause Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22127)
*Devansh Singh,Sundaraparipurnan Narayanan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套面向法律合同条款起草的LLM评估框架，结合CLAUSE分类法与LENS-CRAFT九维评分体系，并采用“最大严重性原则”保留单个致命缺陷的可见性，主张法律AI评估应从聚合准确率转向条款特定、失败模式驱动、风险敏感的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以准确率或偏好为基础的评估方法不适应法律起草场景：条款可能文笔流畅、风格精良，却遗漏关键例外条款、以不可执行的方式分配风险、错误假设管辖权，或使当事人面临监管责任。因此需要专门针对法律条款缺陷的评估框架。

Method: 对四个模型（Claude Haiku 4.5、Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite、GPT 5.4 Nano、Qwen 3.5 Flash）在22个合同条款类别和34个法律驱动的失败模式下开展实证研究设计。采用两套框架：CLAUSE按法律功能和失败目标对提示进行分类；LENS-CRAFT沿九个法律质量维度为输出打分。不以维度评分求平均，而是应用最大严重性原则，使单个具有法律决定性的缺陷不被平均化掩盖。论文给出评估协议、分类法、分析计划及实证结果报告的结构。

Result: 论文提供了完整的评估协议、分类法与结果报告结构，尚未报告具体实证数据，而是从方法学上论证了条款特定、失败模式驱动、风险敏感评估的必要性与设计。

Conclusion: 法律AI评估应超越聚合准确率，转向条款特定、失败模式驱动、风险敏感的评估方法，以更准确地捕捉LLM生成合同条款中的法律风险。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to draft contractual language, yet conventional accuracy or preference-based evaluations are poorly matched to legal drafting. A clause may be fluent and stylistically polished while still omitting an essential carve-out, allocating risk in an unenforceable way, assuming an inapplicable jurisdiction, or exposing a party to regulatory liability. This paper presents a empirical study design and framework for evaluating LLM-generated contract clauses. The study evaluates four models - Claude Haiku 4.5, Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite, GPT 5.4 Nano, and Qwen 3.5 Flash, across 22 contract clause categories and 34 legally-motivated failure modes. We combine two evaluation frameworks: CLAUSE, which classifies prompts by legal function and failure target, and LENS-CRAFT, which scores outputs across nine legal-quality dimensions. Instead of averaging dimension scores, the study applies a Max Severity Principle so that a single legally decisive defect remains visible. The paper provides the evaluation protocol, taxonomy, analysis plan, and a results structure for reporting empirical findings. We argue that legal AI evaluation should move beyond aggregate accuracy toward clause-specific, failure-mode-driven, and risk-sensitive assessment.

</details>


### [71] [Correlation-Aware Structured Pruning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22131)
*Sicheng Xu,Hao Shi,Wei Zhang,Haoran Pang,Zhenyu Ming,Hao Wu,Zhongyi Huang,Xin Yao,Gong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种相关性感知的结构化剪枝方法，将剪枝目标建模为带基数约束的二元二次规划，显式建模剪枝单元间的交叉依赖，并用依赖感知的贪心交互算法和梯度策略分别优化单元选择与逐层稀疏度分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化剪枝方法通常独立评估可剪枝单元（如通道或注意力头）的重要性，隐含假设剪枝误差可加。但模型权重非正交且单元激活存在强相关，导致该独立性假设失效，引发性能下降。

Method: 提出 Correlation-Aware Structured Pruning 方法：将剪枝目标形式化为带基数约束的二元二次规划（BQP），显式建模重构误差中的跨单元依赖；由于 BQP 是 NP 难问题，开发基于依赖感知边际代价的贪心交互算法进行单元选择；并引入基于梯度的策略实现全模型自适应逐层稀疏度分配。

Result: 在主流 LLM 上的大量实验表明，引入相关性信息相比于代表性的结构化剪枝基线，实现了有竞争力的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模单元间的非正交性与激活相关性，相关性感知剪枝能够缓解独立性假设带来的性能损失，显著改善结构化剪枝的精度-效率权衡。

Abstract: Structured pruning is a promising approach for reducing the substantial inference costs of Large Language Models (LLMs) while maintaining hardware efficiency. Many existing methods assess the importance of prunable units (e.g., channels or heads) in isolation, implicitly assuming that pruning errors are additive. This independence assumption is often invalidated by the non-orthogonality of model weights and strong correlations between unit activations, potentially leading to performance degradation. To address this, we propose a Correlation-Aware Structured Pruning method. We formulate the pruning objective as a cardinality-constrained binary quadratic program that explicitly models cross-unit dependencies in the reconstruction error. Since this binary quadratic program is NP-hard and difficult to solve exactly, we develop a greedy interaction algorithm based on dependency-aware marginal costs to optimize unit selection. Furthermore, we incorporate a gradient-based strategy to achieve adaptive layer-wise sparsity allocation across the entire model. Extensive experiments on mainstream LLMs demonstrate that incorporating correlation information yields competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-offs compared to representative structured pruning baselines.

</details>


### [72] [Observational Equivalence of LLM and Human Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22133)
*Kentaro Nakamura,Jing Ling Tan,George Yean*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过复现14项同行评议政治学研究的文本分类任务，发现近期大语言模型（LLM）与专家编码者的一致率，与专家之间的相互一致率相当，即二者在标注质量上是"观察等价"的。这种等价源于文本和编码规则本身的模糊性。因此，文本标注的核心挑战已不再是"选人还是选机器"，而是如何制定减少歧义的编码规则并处理残余歧义。作者提出用LLM间的分歧识别困难案例、改进编码手册，并为无法唯一定义标注的文本构建"歧义感知"的下游推断界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治学等社会科学研究长期依赖人工编码文本，但成本高、速度慢且质量受主观因素影响。尽管LLM展现出接近甚至匹敌人类专家的编码能力，学界对"用LLM替代人工编码是否可靠"仍存疑虑。作者旨在通过大规模、可复现的实证比较，系统检验LLM与人类专家在标注质量上是否真正等效，并揭示等效背后的机制，从而为编码实践提供理论依据与操作方案。

Method: 作者复现了14项同行评议政治学研究中的文本分类任务，让10个大语言模型、3位人类专家和165名众包工人使用完全相同的编码手册（codebook）独立标注相同文本。通过比较LLM与专家之间、专家之间、众包工人之间的一致率（agreement rate），量化标注质量等价性。为验证机制，作者分析分歧是否源于文本与规则的歧义：观察当LLM与专家分歧时专家之间是否也更容易分歧，并测试澄清编码规则能否同时降低专家和足够强大LLM的分歧。最后，提出两种方法：利用LLM间的分歧识别困难案例以迭代改进编码手册；构建歧义感知的统计界限，用于在无法对每段文本唯一定义标注时约束下游推断。

Result: 近期LLM与专家编码者的一致率与专家之间的一致率相当，证明二者在标注质量上观察等价。这种等价由文本和编码规则的歧义驱动：当LLM与专家分歧时，专家之间也更容易分歧；且澄清编码规则能同时降低专家与足够强大LLM的分歧。因此，仅就标注质量而言，几乎没有实证依据支持偏袒人工编码，而LLM在速度和成本上具有显著优势。作者提出的"LLM间分歧识别难例"策略和"歧义感知边界"方法，可有效改进编码手册并为残余歧义下的下游统计推断提供保障。

Conclusion: 文本标注的核心挑战已从"人类编码与机器编码之间的选择"转变为"制定最小化歧义的编码规则，并对剩余的歧义进行恰当处理"。LLM在保持与专家相当的标注质量的同时，具备显著的速度与成本优势，因而没有理由仅凭标注质量偏好人工编码。作者主张将研究重点转向编码规则的歧义治理：通过LLM间分歧来定位并修订规则，并用歧义感知的推断边界来处理无法唯一标注的文本。

Abstract: In this paper, we show that LLM and human coding are observationally equivalent in terms of annotation quality: recent LLMs agree with expert coders at rates comparable to those observed among experts themselves. We demonstrate this through replications of text-classification tasks from 14 peer-reviewed political science studies, in which ten LLMs, three human experts, and 165 crowdsourced workers independently classify the same texts using identical codebooks. We find that this equivalence is driven by ambiguity in the texts and coding rules. When LLMs disagree with experts, experts are also more likely to disagree with one another, and clarifying coding rules reduces disagreement among both experts and sufficiently capable LLMs. Thus, there is little empirical basis for preferring human coding on the basis of annotation quality alone, while LLMs offer substantial advantages in speed and cost. We therefore argue that the central challenge of text annotation is no longer choosing between human and machine coders, but developing coding rules that minimize ambiguity and accounting for the ambiguity that remains. To this end, we propose using disagreement across LLMs to identify difficult cases and refine codebooks, and we develop ambiguity-aware bounds for downstream inference when a unique annotation cannot be defined for every text.

</details>


### [73] [Read-Best Is Not Steer-Best: A Probing--Steering Layer Dissociation in Omni-Modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22135)
*Yibo Wang,Jisheng Dang,Bimei Wang,Yitao Wu,Wencan Zhang,Hong Peng,Jizhao Liu,Bin Hu,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次对"探针准确率最高的层即激活引导最佳层"这一常见假设进行因果检验，发现该假设在三个全模态模型上均不成立，存在"探针-引导层分离"现象；引导有效的层稳定落在归一化深度的中后段，并以双因素机制（可读性与下游可塑性）加以解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态大模型研究中，常默认"探针表现最好的层就是最适合做激活引导的层"，但这一假设缺乏因果层面的严格验证。作者以情绪为受控测试平台，检验"读（探针）"与"写（引导）"是否依赖相同层。

Method: 在三个独立开发的全模态模型上，对文本、音频、图像输入逐层测量可读性（线性探针准确率）与可引导性（激活引导的因果效果）；采用随机方向配对对照以排除扰动与方向质量的干扰；用logit-lens分析前向过程并划分阶段；并识别跨模态情绪子空间（以效价与唤醒度组织，以"快乐"为稳定锚点）。

Result: 探针最佳层在不同架构间差异很大，而引导有效层稳定集中在归一化深度的中后段窄区间；随机方向对照显示约26倍的因果差距，排除了随机扰动和方向质量两种解释；logit-lens揭示"因果手柄→探针饱和→词汇承诺"的分阶段前向过程；验证了双因素机制（可读性+下游可塑性）决定引导有效性。

Conclusion: 探针准确率是选择干预层的劣质启发式指标；建议采用跨架构的中后段层选择准则；情绪可通过效价与唤醒度组织的跨模态子空间表示，"快乐"作为跨模型的稳定锚点。代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models integrate text, audio, and image signals into a shared residual stream, where concepts such as emotion can be linearly decoded and causally modified by activation steering. A common but rarely tested assumption is that the layer with the highest probing accuracy is also the best layer for steering, so injection layers are often selected by probe performance. We provide the first causal test of this assumption across three independently developed omni-modal models and find that it fails. Reading and intervention rely on different layers, a phenomenon we call the probing-steering layer dissociation. Using emotion as a controlled testbed, we measure layer-wise readability and steerability across text, audio, and image inputs. Probe-best layers vary widely across architectures, while steering-effective layers consistently fall within a narrow mid-to-late range of normalized depth. Paired random-direction controls show an approximately 26-fold causal gap, ruling out random perturbation and direction quality as explanations. Logit-lens analysis reveals a staged forward process: causal handle, probing saturation, and vocabulary commitment, and motivates a two-factor account in which steering effectiveness depends on both representational readability and downstream plasticity. These results show that probing accuracy is a poor heuristic for selecting intervention layers and suggest a cross-architecture mid-to-late selection criterion. We also identify a cross-modal emotion subspace organized by valence and arousal, with joy acting as a stable anchor across models. Code and data: https://github.com/YiboWang2002/Read-Best-Is-Not-Steer-Best.

</details>


### [74] [DiFA: Dual Evidence Fusion and Aggregation for Token-Level Text Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22136)
*Yanyu Qian,Pengcheng Weng,Yue Tan,Enguang Zuo,Yu Zheng,Yixin Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 DiFA（双证据框架，含自适应融合与聚合），用于文本 token 级异常检测，结合形式-结构与语义两类互补证据，并在多基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本异常检测大多只能做文档级检测，难以定位有害短语或支持针对性预防；而新兴的 token 级方法（代表性工作）主要依赖表示空间距离度量，忽视了不同异常线索在捕捉多样化异常模式上的互补作用。

Method: 提出 DiFA 框架：1) 分别从形式-结构视角（捕捉可见的结构异常）和语义视角（捕捉上下文不一致）计算异常分数，提供互补证据；2) 设计校准与融合机制，自适应平衡两个尺度不同的分数；3) 设计多变量聚合方法，从多角度汇总 token 级异常分数得到文档级分数，避免稀有异常 token 被稀释。

Result: 在多个文本异常检测基准上的大量实验表明，DiFA 持续取得最优性能，同时保持较强的效率、鲁棒性和可解释性。

Conclusion: DiFA 通过双证据（形式-结构与语义）融合与多变量聚合，有效解决了 token 级文本异常检测中线索单一和异常 token 稀释的问题，在性能、效率、鲁棒性与可解释性上均表现优异，代码已开源。

Abstract: Text anomaly detection, the task of identifying text instances that deviate from normal language patterns, is crucial for language-driven applications. However, most existing methods can only perform document-level anomaly detection, making it hard to locate harmful phrases or support targeted prevention. Recently, there has been an emerging trend toward token-level text anomaly detection, which aims to address the above limitation by identifying anomalous words or fragments within a document. Nevertheless, one representative method mainly relies on representation-space distance measurement, neglecting the complementary roles of different anomaly cues in capturing diverse abnormal patterns. To bridge the gaps, we propose a Dual-evidence framework with adaptive Fusion and Aggregation (DiFA) for token-level anomaly detection. DiFA derives anomaly scores from form-structural and semantic views to capture visible structural abnormality and contextual inconsistency, respectively, thereby providing complementary evidence for identifying diverse anomalies. To combine these two scores with varying numerical scales, DiFA incorporates a calibration and fusion mechanism to adaptively balance the two views. Moreover, to obtain a discriminative document-level score, a multivariate aggregation method is designed to summarize token-level anomaly scores from multiple perspectives, preventing rare anomalous tokens from being diluted. Extensive experiments across various text anomaly detection benchmarks demonstrate that DiFA consistently achieves top performance while maintaining strong efficiency, robustness, and interpretability. The code and scripts are available at: https://github.com/qyy11-com/DiFA.

</details>


### [75] [Does the Truthfulness Signal Survive Code-Mixing? Probing Hidden States for Hallucination Detection in Hinglish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22138)
*Tanveer Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究幻觉探测探针在印地语-英语混合语 ("Hinglish") 上的迁移能力，发现幻觉信号在语码混合场景下表现良好（迁移 AUROC 0.88–0.99），且印地语训练的探针比英语训练的探针迁移更可靠；同时发现三个模型在印地语和 Hinglish 上的幻觉率远高于英语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的隐性状态幻觉探测研究（在 LLM 内部激活上训练线性分类器）已在多个基准和语言上取得 0.90–1.00 的 AUROC，但从未在语码混合输入（尤其印地语-英语的 "Hinglish"）上测试过，而大量聊天机器人用户实际使用 Hinglish。本文直接填补这一空白，探究在纯净语言上训练的幻觉探针能否迁移到 Hinglish，信号是否会因语码混合而退化。

Method: 构建了一个 5,674 条目的印地语/英语/Hinglish 问答基准，在三个开源 7-8B LLM（Qwen2.5-7B、Mistral-7B、Llama-3.1-8B）上生成并标注 17,022 条模型回答；在每个 token 位置提取逐层隐藏状态，训练线性和 MLP 探针用于分布内检测和跨语言迁移，并对比印地语训练与英语训练的探针向 Hinglish 的迁移表现。

Result: 幻觉信号在语码混合下保存良好：迁移 AUROC 介于 0.88 到 0.99，与分布内性能的差距大多小于 0.05 AUROC；印地语训练的探针迁移到 Hinglish 比英语训练的探针更可靠。作为独立的实用发现，在匹配事实条件下，三个模型在印地语和 Hinglish 上的幻觉率都显著高于英语。代码和合成 Hinglish 问答数据集已公开。

Conclusion: 在纯净语言上训练的幻觉探测探针可以较好地迁移到 Hinglish 语码混合文本，表明幻觉信号对语码混合具有鲁棒性，且印地语探针是更优先的迁移源；同时揭示了非英语及混合语言场景下模型幻觉更严重的问题，为后续语码混合幻觉检测研究提供了数据与代码支持。

Abstract: Hidden-state hallucination probing - training a linear classifier on an LLM's internal activations to detect whether a generated answer is faithful to the input - is an active area of 2026 research, with recent work reporting 0.90-1.00 AUROC across several benchmarks and languages. However, none of this work has tested probes on code-mixed input, despite the fact that a huge population of chatbot users write in Hindi-English code-mixed text ("Hinglish"). We address this gap directly: does a hallucination probe trained on clean-language hidden states transfer to Hinglish, or does the signal degrade under code-mixing? We construct a 5,674-item Hindi/English/Hinglish QA benchmark, generate and label 17,022 model responses across three open-weight 7-8B LLMs (Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Llama-3.1-8B), extract per-layer hidden states at two token positions, and train linear and MLP probes for in-distribution detection and cross-lingual transfer. We find that the hallucination signal survives code-mixing well: transfer AUROC ranges from 0.88 to 0.99, with gaps of mostly under 0.05 AUROC relative to in-distribution performance, and that Hindi-trained probes transfer to Hinglish more reliably than English-trained probes. As an independent, practically motivated finding, all three models hallucinate substantially more on Hindi and Hinglish than on English for matched facts. We release our code and synthetic Hinglish QA dataset to support further work on code-mixed hallucination detection.

</details>


### [76] [Using Composition Operators to Linearize LLM Semantic Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22143)
*Afjal Chowdhury,James Chen,Alan Edelman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出用动力系统中的Koopman主义（特别是复合算子/Composition Operators）来刻画大语言模型（LLM）的数学结构，将LLM视为矩形无穷维算子，并利用其奇异值谱来比较任务与模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习本质上在学习函数（如提示词到响应的映射、图像到字幕的映射），但这些函数的数学本质目前难以描述。作者希望借助动力系统领域的Koopman算子理论，为LLM等变换提供严格的数学形式化框架。

Method: 引入复合算子（composition operators）以推广Koopman算子，使其能够在不同空间之间映射，从而将LLM变换理解为矩形无穷维算子。在该形式下，当提示词与响应分布满足自然假设时，LLM算子为等距算子（isometry）；同时，学习表示间的失配（misalignment）表现为其有限截面（finite sections）的谱污染（spectral pollution）。此外，给出构造LLM算子有限维近似的方法。

Result: 证明在自然分布假设下LLM算子是等距的；将表示失配与有限截面谱污染联系起来；并提出用奇异值谱（singular value spectrum）比较不同任务和模型的有限维近似方案。

Conclusion: 通过Koopman主义中的复合算子框架，可以将LLM的变换形式化为矩形无穷维算子，从而借助算子理论工具（等距性、谱污染、奇异值谱）对其进行分析与比较，为理解机器学习函数的数学本质提供了新视角。

Abstract: Machine learning learns functions: prompt to response, image to caption. What these functions are mathematically remains hard to say. We present a method to approximate these kinds of transformations using techniques from dynamical systems that fall under the umbrella of Koopmanism. We introduce the use of composition operators, which generalize the Koopman operator and, crucially, can map between distinct spaces, motivating the perspective that LLM transformations are rectangular infinite-dimensional operators. This formalism reveals useful structure: under natural assumptions on the prompt and response distributions, the LLM operator is an isometry, and misalignment between learned representations manifests as spectral pollution of its finite sections. We then outline a method of constructing finite-dimensional approximations of an LLM operator, and demonstrate how the singular value spectrum can be used to compare tasks and models.

</details>


### [77] [Multilingual Safety Signals Are Multi-Layered: Filtering Safety-Degrading Data for Safer LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22144)
*Jiakun Li,Guowei Song,Sijia Li,Xingwei He,Hongzheng Chai,Yuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MMSAFE，一种基于多层表征的多语言安全退化数据识别框架。作者发现安全敏感层在多语言模型中仅部分跨语言共享，安全信号分散于多层，因此利用多层建模来捕捉共享与语言特有的安全信号，显著降低了有害响应比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常仅依赖单一安全敏感层的表征来识别微调数据中的安全退化样本，这在单语场景有效，但多语言模型中的安全敏感层可能因跨语言表征差异而未被充分共享，单层假设在多语言场景下的有效性存疑。

Method: 通过跨语言分析发现安全敏感层仅部分共享、安全信号分散于多层后，提出 MMSAFE 多层级框架，综合利用多层表征同时捕捉跨语言共享的安全信号和各语言特有的安全信号，用于识别多语言安全退化数据。

Result: 在多个模型、语言和安全基准上的实验表明，MMSAFE 相比随机过滤将平均有害响应比例降低 60%，且平均性能优于最强的单层基线方法。

Conclusion: 多层级建模能够稳健地提升多语言安全对齐效果，验证了利用多层表征捕捉共享与语言特有安全信号在多语言场景下优于单层方法。

Abstract: Preserving safety alignment during large language models fine-tuning is critical, however, recent studies have demonstrated that even benign fine-tuning data may contain safety-degrading samples that silently undermine safety alignment. Existing approaches typically identify such samples using representations from a single safety-sensitive layer. While this assumption has shown effectiveness in monolingual settings, its validity for multilingual models remains unclear due to potential cross-lingual differences in representation patterns. Through a cross-lingual analysis, we show that sensitive layers are only partially shared across languages, with safety-relevant signals often distributed across multiple layers. Motivated by these observations, we propose MMSAFE, a multi-layer framework for multilingual safety-degrading data identification that captures both shared and language-specific safety signals. Extensive experiments across multiple models, languages, and safety benchmarks demonstrate that MMSAFE reduces the average harmful-response ratio by 60% compared with random filtering and achieves stronger average performance than the strongest single-layer baseline, demonstrating the effectiveness of multi-layer modeling for robust multilingual safety alignment.

</details>


### [78] [Beyond the Stitching Assumption: A Unified Framework for Multimodal Synthetic Data Evaluation via Semantic Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22149)
*Yefeng Yuan,Zhan Shi,Liang Cheng,Yuhong Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于投影的表格—文本合成数据跨模态评估器，通过句嵌入+k-means聚类、类别/分箱状态将两种模态投影到联合状态空间，用JSD、NMI、cJSD、联合状态熵等指标并结合文本排列对照（permutation baseline）来检测跨模态配对是否被打乱，实验表明单一模态指标在破坏配对后仍保持高分，而投影诊断能有效识别此类破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法往往将多模态合成数据中的结构化属性与自由文本分开评估，这些单一模态指标即使在表格与文本配对关系被打乱后仍可能保持高分，无法反映跨模态配对的真实质量，因此需要显式的跨模态评估方法与排列基线。

Method: 使用固定句子编码器将文本映射为嵌入，再用k-means转换为聚类状态；表格变量表示为类别状态或分位数分箱状态。对真实与合成数据的列联表，计算Jensen-Shannon散度（JSD）、归一化互信息（NMI）、条件JSD（cJSD）及联合状态熵；同时报告文本到属性（T2A）效用以及经留出集校准的邻近标志率（PFR）作为表示层诊断。引入文本排列对照，该对照保持两个边际分布不变但打乱其配对关系。

Result: 在Amazon Reviews、Kiva Loans和Employment Scam Aegean Dataset上的实验表明，单一模态评分在文本排列对照下依然保持高分；当真实投影依赖超过排列基线时，投影诊断能够检测到配对破坏，但对弱依赖或稀疏投影则不敏感。一些带条件的LLM基线甚至表现出比真实数据投影更强的测量依赖。这些结果支持结合排列基线与覆盖报告的显式跨模态评估。

Conclusion: 文章主张采用显式的跨模态评估方法，并以排列基线和覆盖报告作为补充，因为单一模态指标无法反映表格—文本配对被打乱的情况，投影诊断在依赖较强时有效，但对弱或稀疏投影的局限也需报告。

Abstract: Multimodal synthetic datasets combine structured attributes with free text, but are often evaluated separately. Such metrics can remain high after tabular--text pairings are disrupted. We present a projection-based evaluator for tabular--text synthetic data. A fixed sentence encoder maps text to embeddings, \(k\)-means converts them to cluster states, and tabular variables are represented as categorical or quantile-binned states. Real and synthetic contingency tables are compared using Jensen--Shannon divergence (JSD), normalized mutual information (NMI), conditional JSD (cJSD), and joint-state entropy. We also report text-to-attribute (T2A) utility and a holdout-calibrated proximity flag rate (PFR) as a representation-level diagnostic. A text-permutation control preserves both marginal distributions while disrupting their pairing. Experiments on Amazon Reviews, Kiva Loans, and the Employment Scam Aegean Dataset show that modality-specific scores remain high under this control. The projection diagnostics detect disruption when real projected dependence exceeds a permutation baseline, but are less informative for weak or sparse projections. Some conditioned LLM baselines also exhibit stronger measured dependence than the corresponding real-data projections. These results support explicit cross-modal evaluation with permutation baselines and coverage reporting.

</details>


### [79] [Do Language Models Know Their Own Constraints?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22151)
*Arin Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究经 LoRA 微调（SFT 与 GRPO）习得的行为约束是否仍能被模型明确报告。结果显示，两种方法均将合规率从 4% 提升至约 90%，但显著降低了对约束的显式报告能力和第三人称保留知识，且 GRPO（基于奖励的信号）更具破坏性；即便加入正对照，模型仍无法在要求时枚举约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究通过后训练获得的行为约束是否以内省可及、可明确报告的形式存储于模型中，而非仅表现为隐式的、不可言说的行为改变。

Method: 以"受限食谱生成"为测试平台，使用 Llama 3.1 8B Instruct，通过 LoRA 微调强行禁止五种食材。对比监督微调（SFT）与分组相对策略优化（GRPO）两种方法与未训练基线，在四层级"约束意识基准"上评估：行为合规率、显式约束报告、第三人称知识保留；并引入上下文条件奖励（试图教会"自我-他人"区分）和显式自我描述示例（正对照），同时探测提示时隐藏状态以恢复逐食材的回避信息。

Result: 两种方法均将行为合规率从 4% 提升到约 90%，但显式约束报告降至 0.16/5（SFT）和 0.07/5（GRPO），均低于未训练模型（0.48/5）；第三人称知识从 93% 降至 36%（SFT）与 14%（GRPO），方法间差异显著（p<0.01）。奖励信号更具破坏性：惩罚禁用食材 token（无论上下文）的奖励学到了与上下文无关的抑制，而非自我导向的约束；上下文条件奖励则失败，两个框架中均向"包含"坍塌。探测隐藏状态对逐食材回避的恢复率为 83.8%（第24层 MLP），仅比逐食材基础率预测器（77.4%）高 6.4 个百分点，而模型自身口头自我报告更准（87.8%）。加入显式自我描述示例的正对照未能恢复报告能力。

Conclusion: 失败特定于"按需枚举约束"，而非一般性地丧失对约束的访问。模型虽具备行为合规和一定程度的隐式知识，但无法将习得的行为约束显式报告出来，且基于奖励的 GRPO 对可报告性的侵蚀更为严重。

Abstract: We ask whether behavioral constraints acquired through post training remain explicitly reportable. Using constrained recipe generation as a testbed, five banned ingredients enforced via LoRA fine tuning of Llama 3.1 8B Instruct we compare supervised fine tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) against an untrained baseline on a four tier Constraint Awareness Benchmark. Averaged over three seeds, both methods raise behavioral compliance from 4% to about 90% while reducing explicit constraint reporting below the untrained model (0.48/5 to 0.16/5 for SFT, 0.07/5 for GRPO) and eroding retained third person knowledge (93% to 36% for SFT, 14% for GRPO; p less than 0.01 between methods). Contrary to our initial hypothesis, the reward based signal is the more destructive of the two: a reward that penalizes banned ingredient tokens regardless of framing learns a context independent suppression rather than a self directed constraint. A context conditioned reward designed to teach the self to other distinction fails, collapsing toward inclusion in both framings. Probing prompt time hidden states recovers per ingredient avoidance at 83.8% (layer 24 MLP), but only 6.4 points above a per ingredient base rate predictor (77.4%), and the model's own verbal self report is more accurate still (87.8%). A positive control adding explicit self description examples does not restore reporting. The failure is therefore specific to enumerating constraints on request, not a general loss of access to them.

</details>


### [80] [Is Imagination Derived from Hallucination? A Cross-Taxonomy Evaluation of Imagination and Hallucination in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22152)
*Zixuan Tang,Hongzong Li,Shuxin Zhuang,Dapeng Wu,Zi Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Whiteboard，首个针对大语言模型想象力的评测基准，将七种机制导向的想象力子类型与十种支撑边界幻觉子类型在同一生成上联合打分，并以显式支撑检查门控想象力分数，发现幻觉与想象力大多呈负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有创造力基准只衡量输出偏离常见答案的程度，却不检查偏离是否由提示词授权；而幻觉作为想象力的近邻，总是在独立流程、不同生成上测量，导致"想象与幻觉同源"这一说法从未被直接检验。

Method: 借鉴认知科学中测量人类想象力的权威工具，将经典范式改编为七种机制导向的想象力子类型，与十种支撑边界幻觉子类型交叉，在同一生成上联合打分。每次想象力得分都经过显式支撑检查门控，并通过可审计的原子矩阵确定性计算两个轴，主路径不使用 LLM 裁判。基准含 1,660 条提示，在共享的 80 条锚定集上评估 79 个先进 LLM，并以 13,280 条人类判断进行校验。

Result: 幻觉与想象力之间存在反直觉的负相关：大多数子类型耦合为负，且每一条锚定项自身都复现了这种负耦合。

Conclusion: Whiteboard 提供了可审计、以支撑检查为门控的想象力评测工具，实证表明想象力并不必然与幻觉同源，反而在多数子类型上与幻觉呈负相关，为理解 LLM 的生成机制提供了新证据。

Abstract: Imagination performs as a high-level function of large language models (LLMs) which determines the potential of how an LLM creates unseen or creative content. While existing works have built a rich family of creativity benchmarks for this ability, they only measure how far an output departs from common answers and never check whether the departure is licensed by the prompt. Moreover, hallucination, the closest neighbor of imagination, is always measured in a separate pipeline on different generations, so the influential claim that imagination and hallucination stem from the same generative mechanism has never been directly testable. In this paper, we propose Whiteboard, the first LLM imagination evaluation benchmark. Its design follows the authoritative cognitive instruments developed to measure human imagination: seven mechanism-grounded imagination subtypes are adapted from classic paradigms, then crossed with ten support-boundary hallucination subtypes and scored jointly on the same generation. Different from previous creativity or hallucination benchmarks, Whiteboard gates every imagination score with an explicit support check and computes both axes deterministically through an auditable atom matrix, with no LLM judge on the primary path. The full Whiteboard item bank contains 1,660 prompts; on its shared 80-item anchor set, we evaluate 79 state-of-the-art LLMs and validate the instrument against 13,280 human judgments. Additionally, we further explore whether imagination derives from the same generative tendency as hallucination and what key factors shape it. Our analysis indicates a counterintuitive correlation between hallucination and imagination: Most of the subtype couplings are negative, every one of the anchor items reproduces the negative coupling on its own.

</details>


### [81] [Beyond Raw Context Transfer: Representation-based Federated Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22162)
*Can Peng,Yu Liu,Yingyu Yang,Anjie Le,Yuyuan Liu,Qianye Yang,J. Alison Noble*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FedRepRAG 是一种基于表示的去中心化 RAG 框架，原始文档留在各客户端，跨客户端检索时仅交换紧凑的潜在表示，并通过协同训练的投影器将检索嵌入转换为生成器可用的表示令牌，从而兼顾效果与隐私/效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 多依赖集中式检索语料库，在医疗等敏感领域不切实际；而去中心化 RAG 的提示式范式需交换原始可读内容，带来推理计算开销大和检索信息直接暴露的问题。

Method: 提出 Representation-based Federated RAG（FedRepRAG）：原始文档保留在所属客户端本地；跨客户端仅交换紧凑潜在表示；引入协同训练的 projector，将检索嵌入转换为兼容冻结 LLM/VLM 主干的表示令牌。

Result: 在去中心化 VQA 与 QA 基准上，FedRepRAG 一致优于直接推理和本地检索基线；相比原始上下文传输，显著缩短检索上下文长度并降低推理计算开销。

Conclusion: FedRepRAG 在不传输原始检索内容的前提下，为联邦 RAG 提供了有效且高效的框架；进一步分析证实了查询相关检索表示的重要性，并刻画了表示交换带来的残余表示级泄漏。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves the factuality of large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) by grounding generation in external knowledge. However, most existing RAG frameworks assume a centralized retrieval corpus, which is often impractical in sensitive domains such as healthcare, where data are inherently distributed and raw content cannot be directly shared across institutions. Recent efforts on decentralized RAG primarily follow prompt-based paradigms that exchange raw, human-readable retrieved content, leading to substantial inference-time computational overhead and direct exposure of retrieved information. To address these limitations, we propose Representation-based Federated RAG (FedRepRAG), a decentralized RAG framework that keeps raw documents at their owning clients and exchanges only compact latent representations during cross-client retrieval. To integrate retrieved knowledge, we introduce a collaboratively trained projector that converts retrieval embeddings into generator-compatible representation tokens for a frozen LLM/VLM backbone. Experiments across decentralized visual question answering (VQA) and question answering (QA) benchmarks show that FedRepRAG consistently outperforms direct inference and local retrieval baselines while substantially reducing retrieval-context length and inference-time computational overhead compared with raw-context transfer. Further analyses confirm the importance of query-relevant retrieved representations and characterize the residual representation-level leakage associated with representation exchange. Overall, FedRepRAG provides an effective and efficient framework for federated RAG without transferring raw retrieved content.

</details>


### [82] [MechaTerp-TRACE: A Novel Approach for Component Ablation Analysis in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22163)
*Brandon Colelough,Davis Bartels,Madeline Bittner,Dina Demner-Fushman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MechaTerp-TRACE，一种统一测量语言模型中各类组件对特定命名实体生成因果贡献的消融评测方法，并在13个模型（1-30B参数）上对49,656个组件进行消融，发现承载最大效应的始终是少数几个位置固定的通用生成组件，实体知识并不集中定位，这对定向知识编辑等方法有直接警示意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性研究分别揭示了前馈网络中的事实回忆与自注意力中的token关系，但缺乏统一框架来比较不同架构组件对模型输出的因果贡献。作者希望弥合这一空白，为不同粒度（从整个transformer块到单个神经元和输出logits）的组件建立可比较的因果效应度量，并检验"实体知识是否定位在可找到的位置"这一假设。

Method: 设计MechaTerp-TRACE（注册组件消融效应的教师强制式记录子集）：每次仅消融一个组件，在固定答案token上测量输出分布的变化，使不同组件类型可在同一尺度上比较因果贡献。应用于13个经过指令微调的稠密解码器模型（覆盖5个模型家族、1B至30B参数），对48个医学提示和42个通用知识提示下的49,656个组件进行了消融。

Result: 研究发现：在所有模型中，承载最大效应的都是少数几个位置固定的相同组件，与提示询问的实体无关；移除这些组件后，其余支持在13个模型中的11个里几乎均匀分布。表明实体知识的表面局部化主要归因于通用生成机制，而非实体知识本身被集中存储。

Conclusion: 实体知识在被测模型中并不集中在一个可定位的位置，其表观局部化源于通用生成组件。这对假设实体知识位于可找到位置的方法（尤其是定向知识编辑）产生直接后果，提示此类方法的隐含前提可能不成立。

Abstract: Interpretability research on large language models has produced accounts of factual recall in feed-forward layers and of token relationships in self-attention, but little work offers a unified way to compare the causal contribution of different architecture components to a model's output. We introduce MechaTerp (the Mechanistic Interpretability suite) -TRACE (subset for Teacher-forced Registry of Ablated Component Effects), an architecture and study that measures how much each registered component of a language model supports the production of a named entity. TRACE ablates one component at a time and measures the resulting change in the output distribution at a fixed answer token, so component types from whole transformer blocks down to individual neurons and output logits can be compared on a common scale. We apply it to thirteen instruction-tuned dense decoder models spanning five families and one to thirty billion parameters, ablating 49,656 components across 48 medical and 42 general-knowledge prompts. We find that the components carrying the most effect are the same few, positionally fixed components in every model, regardless of which entity a prompt asks about, and that once these are removed, the remaining support is close to evenly spread in eleven of the thirteen models. Apparent localisation of entity knowledge is therefore largely attributable to generic generation machinery, which has direct consequences for methods that assume entity knowledge sits in a findable place, including targeted knowledge editing.

</details>


### [83] [A Multi-Agent Pipeline for Source-Grounded Synthetic Note Generation from Longitudinal Structured EHR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22164)
*Nina Fatehi,Reihaneh Hassanzadeh,Meysam Ghaffari,Animesh Agarwal,Carlos Morato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedNotes 是一个多智能体合成数据生成流水线，将纵向结构化 EHR 转化为有源可溯的临床笔记，并通过评估-路由闭环实现质量控制；在 EHRSHOT 上通过率 91.4%，且合成语料增强了 CPT 预测与段落级章节预测等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化 EHR 数据虽丰富但稀疏、带编码、难以直接用于以笔记为中心的临床建模，需要一种把结构化数据高质量转化为临床笔记表示的方法。

Method: MedNotes 将结构数据到文本的合成建模为闭环智能体流程：生成器起草笔记，评估器智能体诊断事实性、覆盖率、结构性和幻觉类错误，路由器据此接受、修订或拒绝草稿，并支持迭代精炼。

Result: 在 1,485 个 EHRSHOT 就诊记录上，MedNotes 通过率 91.4%，平均事实准确率 0.980，完整度 99.1%，结构保真度 0.761，每次就诊 0.028 次严重幻觉；迭代精炼将接受率从 69.4% 提升至 91.4%。合成语料在结合少量真实数据时改进了 CPT 预测与段落级章节预测。

Conclusion: MedNotes 通过闭环节点式质量控制，能够大规模且高保真地将结构化 EHR 转化为源接地临床笔记，其合成语料可有效辅助下游临床任务，缓解高质量标注笔记稀缺的问题。

Abstract: Structured EHR is abundant but sparse, coded, and difficult to use directly for note-centric clinical modeling. We present MedNotes, a multi-agent synthetic data generation pipeline that converts longitudinal structured EHR into source-grounded clinical note representations under explicit quality control. MedNotes treats structured-data-to-text synthesis as a closed-loop agentic process: a generator proposes a note, evaluator agents diagnose factual, coverage, structural, and hallucination-related failures, and a router accepts, revises, or rejects the draft. On 1,485 EHRSHOT encounters, MedNotes achieves a 91.4% pass rate, with mean factual accuracy of 0.980, completeness of 99.1%, structural fidelity of 0.761, and 0.028 critical hallucinations per encounter. Iterative refinement improves acceptance from 69.4% to 91.4%. The resulting synthetic corpus improves downstream CPT prediction and paragraph-level section prediction when combined with limited real data.

</details>


### [84] [Monocultural Biases: Correlated biases in large language models lead to unequal systemic exclusion rates in hiring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22169)
*Matthew Bone,Fabian Stephany,Maria del Rio-Chanona*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在招聘场景中部署大语言模型（LLM）可能引发的"单文化偏见"风险，即多个模型共享同质化偏见、造成系统性排斥。研究者对十个LLM的基座与后训练版本进行了招聘偏见测量，发现后训练模型对年长申请者的回拨概率下降了3.6%，且模型间决策相关性显著提高，导致全局系统性排斥率从5.6%升至17.3%，并加剧了年龄维度的不平等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雇主日益使用LLM实现招聘自动化，这可能导致"单文化偏见"——广泛部署的LLM使偏见在劳动力市场上同质化，从而对特定人口群体形成更大的系统性排斥。作者希望明确这种偏见究竟产生于预训练还是后训练阶段。

Method: 选取十个LLM，分别对其基座模型和后训练版本测量招聘偏见；比较两类模型对年长申请者的回拨率变化、决策间的相关性（共识度），并计算全局及交叉维度的系统性排斥率，以归因偏见来源并量化不平等程度。

Result: 与基座模型相比，后训练模型对年长申请者的回拨概率降低3.6%，十个模型中有八个出现这一负面偏移；后训练模型的决策相关性远高于基座模型，主要受技能、大学专业等人力资本特征驱动。模型间更强的共识使全局系统性排斥率从5.6%升至17.3%，后训练模型的交叉系统性排斥率在12.2%至21.7%之间，且不平等主要由后训练阶段加剧的年龄歧视驱动。

Conclusion: 后训练技术虽然可能提升模型甄选最佳申请者的能力，但也可能通过统一引入新偏见，提高边缘群体的系统性不平等风险。研究提示在部署LLM进行招聘时需警惕后训练带来的同质化偏见及其对弱势群体的系统性排斥。

Abstract: Employers are increasingly using large language models (LLMs) to automate their hiring process. This paper investigates the risk of monocultural biases, in which the widespread deployment of large language models homogenizes biases across the labor market, leading to greater systemic exclusion for certain demographic groups. For ten LLMs, we measure hiring biases across their base and post-trained versions to identify which stage, pre-training or post-training, lead to monocultural biases. We find that, compared to their base models, post-trained models are 3.6% less likely to callback older applicants. This negative shift occurs in eight of the ten models that we evaluate. Post-trained models have much more correlated decisions than base models which is likely driven by human capital traits like skills or college major. However, greater consensus among models increases global systemic exclusion rates from 5.6% to 17.3% and exacerbates demographic inequalities, with intersectional systemic exclusion rates ranging from 12.2% to 21.7% for post-trained models. We find that this inequality is primarily driven by age-based discrimination that is exacerbated in post-training. These results indicate that while post-training techniques may improve models' abilities to select the best applicants, they may raise systemic inequality risks for those at the margin by uniformly introducing new biases.

</details>


### [85] [Quantifying Hidden Salt for Precision Healthcare: Sodium Assessment via Joint-Factor Retrieval and Chain-of-Thought Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22171)
*Mingyu Huang,Weiqing Min,Yuehui Fang,Yuna He,Shuqiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SALT 框架，利用检索增强生成（RAG）评估食谱中的钠含量，以解决精准医疗中膳食钠摄入难以追踪的问题。框架包含 Joint-Factor Embedding Retrieval 检索模块和结构化 4-hop 思维链推理模块，并配套构建了含 54,151 条条目的 SALT54k 数据集，在钠含量估计上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准医疗（如高血压和心血管疾病管理）需要监测膳食钠摄入，但烹饪中酱油、番茄酱等调味品含有"隐藏盐"，且食谱中这些含钠调味品常被省略或描述模糊，导致难以准确追踪，因此需要一种能从食谱中自动评估钠含量的方法。

Method: 提出 SALT（Sodium Assessing & Level Tracking）框架，采用 RAG 架构：1）Joint-Factor Embedding Retrieval 模块，检索具有明确钠含量标注的相似食谱，为后续推理提供上下文参考；2）结构化的 4-hop 思维链（Chain-of-Thought）推理模块，通过多步钠含量估计细化语言模型的模糊评估。同时构建了 SALT54k 数据集，包含 54,151 条条目，标注了 11 种常见调味品的钠含量。

Result: 在 SALT54k 数据集上，SALT 方法取得钠含量估计的最先进（state-of-the-art）性能，并通过额外的真实世界验证证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SALT 框架能有效评估食谱中的钠含量，解决了隐藏盐导致的膳食钠追踪难题，展示了其作为 AI 辅助精准医疗实用方案的潜力。

Abstract: Precision healthcare, particularly for conditions like hypertension and cardiovascular disease, necessitates monitoring of dietary sodium intake. However, tracking this is hindered by the prevalence of hidden salt in cooking, such as sodium in soy sauce and ketchup. While recipes offer a valuable data source for dietary analysis, sodium-rich seasonings are frequently omitted or described ambiguously in instructions. To solve this issue, we propose SALT, a Sodium Assessing & Level Tracking framework adopting an RAG framework to assess sodium content in recipes. Our framework first introduces a Joint-Factor Embedding Retrieval module to locate similar recipes with specified sodium content for addressing the lack of contextual references. These retrieved samples provide contexts for subsequent inference. Then we design a structured 4-hop Chain-of-Thought inference module to refine the vague estimation from language models through a multi-step sodium estimation. To facilitate our study, we further construct a recipe dataset SALT54k with $54,151$ entries labeled with sodium quantities across $11$ common seasonings. Results on SALT54k demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in sodium estimation. Additional real-world validations confirm the effectiveness of our method, demonstrating its potential as a practical solution for AI-assisted precision healthcare.

</details>


### [86] [SCoR: A Hierarchical Framework for Forecasting Relations Between Scientific Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22174)
*Jingze Wang,Fred Sun,Shangqi Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将研究方向发现建模为分层科学关系预测（共现、关系形成、关系类型），构建SCoR-Graph数据集与SCoR-Bench基准，并设计HiSCoR模型，在2025-2026留出窗口上显著优于现有时序图基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要预测概念未来是否共现，但共现只反映概念受到的共同关注，无法刻画连接的科学含义（是使用、组合、替代还是矛盾）。因此需要从"哪些概念会共现"推进到"是否以及如何形成有证据支撑的科学关系"。

Method: 将研究方向发现形式化为共享候选对空间上的分层科学关系预测，包含三个时间对齐的任务：首次共现、首次科学关系形成、形成时的关系类型。构建SCoR-Graph（187,848篇cs.CV论文，2017-2026，270,687个概念、745万共现边、615,036条有类型有向关系边），并生成泄漏审计的SCoR-Bench基准（关系类型测试集经专家验证）。提出HiSCoR模型族，通过编码截断前事件历史、并以未来共现为条件约束关系形成，将关系涌现建模为时间演化、层级约束的过程。

Result: 在留出的2025-2026窗口上，HiSCoR AUROC达0.9515，较最强时序图基线相对提升2.4%，population-AUPRC提升14.0%；关系类型变体Macro-AUROC达0.7795。消融表明语义、共现、有类型关系三种视图提供互补的预测证据。

Conclusion: SCoR将研究方向预测从"预测概念共现"推进到"预测有证据支撑的科学关系是否及如何涌现"，为AI辅助科学的方向发现提供了新的任务框架、基准和模型。

Abstract: Anticipating emerging research directions is a critical goal of AI-assisted science. Existing methods mainly predict which concepts will co-occur in future papers, but co-occurrence captures shared attention rather than the scientific meaning of a connection, such as whether one method uses, combines, replaces, or contradicts another. We formulate research-direction discovery as hierarchical scientific-relation forecasting over a shared candidate-pair space, comprising three temporally aligned tasks: first co-occurrence, first scientific-relation formation, and relation type at formation. We construct SCoR-Graph from 187,848 cs.CV papers published between 2017 and 2026, yielding 270,687 consolidated concepts, 7.45 million co-occurrence edges, and 615,036 typed, directed relation edges. From cutoff-specific graph snapshots, we derive SCoR-Bench, a leakage-audited benchmark for these three capabilities, with expert-verified gold labels for the entire relation-type test set. We further introduce HiSCoR, a task-adapted model family that models relation emergence as a temporally evolving, hierarchically constrained process by encoding pre-cutoff event histories and conditioning relation formation on future co-occurrence. On the held-out 2025-2026 window, HiSCoR achieves an AUROC of 0.9515, a 2.4% relative improvement over the strongest temporal-graph baseline, and improves population-AUPRC by 14.0%; its relation-type variant achieves a Macro-AUROC of 0.7795. Ablations show that semantic, co-occurrence, and typed-relation views provide complementary predictive evidence. SCoR advances research-direction forecasting from predicting which concepts will co-occur to anticipating whether and how evidence-backed scientific relations will emerge.

</details>


### [87] [Fairness Beyond Anonymization? Demographic Leakage in German LLM-Generated Resumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22188)
*Charlotte Leininger,Helena Veit,Matthias Aßenmacher,Andreas Bender*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对德语环境下 LLM 生成的简历进行了两阶段人口统计特征泄漏审计，发现即使经过匿名化和性别中性化处理，模型仍能基于微妙的中性用词差异可靠地区分出简历生成时所使用的男/女性姓名，而族裔相关泄漏相对较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在欧盟 AI 法案将招聘列为高风险领域的背景下，现有研究多聚焦于显性招聘决策的公平性，而忽视了 LLM 自动生成的简历本身是否编码了可恢复的人口统计信息（如性别、族裔）。作者意图填补这一空白，检验"看似中立"的简历生成是否能保留高度可预测的人口统计信号。

Method: 采用两阶段审计方法：(1) 使用 ChatGPT (GPT-4o-mini)、Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite 以及开源 Qwen 3 系列的多个规模(4B、8B、14B)模型，从真实匿名求职匹配档案生成简历，在保持资历不变的前提下系统性地变换性别相关和族裔相关的姓名；(2) 模拟下游简历筛选场景，先对生成的简历进行匿名化和性别中性化处理，再在文本上训练人口统计特征泄漏分类器，检验分类器能否识别生成时使用的姓名关联属性。

Result: 尽管采取了匿名化和性别中性化干预，分类器仍能可靠地区分使用男性姓名和女性姓名生成的简历；这种泄漏并非源于明显的性别化措辞，而是来自德语中语义等价、形式上性别中立词汇在使用上的细微差异。相比之下，与族裔相关的泄漏在所有模型中都相对较弱。

Conclusion: 看似中立的简历生成依然能保留高度可预测的人口统计信号，这对多语言 AI 招聘流程中基于匿名化的公平性干预措施的有效性提出了质疑，提示需要更强有力的去偏与审计手段来保障高风险场景下的公平性与透明度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into AI-assisted hiring pipelines, including automated resume generation and screening. Under the EU AI Act, the hiring domain is classified as high-risk, making fairness and transparency critical requirements. Existing work has primarily focused on explicit hiring decisions, while less attention has been paid to whether generated resumes themselves encode recoverable demographic information. In this work, we conduct a two-stage audit of demographic leakage in German-language LLM-generated resumes. First, we use ChatGPT (GPT-4o-mini), Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite, and multiple scales of the open-weight Qwen 3 model family (4B, 8B, and 14B) to generate resumes from real anonymized job-matching profiles, systematically varying gender- and ethnicity-associated names while holding qualifications constant. Second, we simulate a downstream resume screening scenario, where the generated resumes are first anonymized and gender-neutralized, before demographic leakage classifiers are trained on the resulting texts. We find that, despite these interventions, classifiers reliably distinguish between resumes generated with male and female names. This leakage is not driven by overtly gendered wording, but by subtle differences in the usage of semantically equivalent, formally gender-neutral terms in German. In contrast, ethnicity-related leakage remains comparatively weak across models. Our findings demonstrate that apparently neutral resume generation can still preserve highly predictive demographic signals, raising concerns about anonymization-based fairness interventions in multilingual AI hiring pipelines.

</details>


### [88] [The Situated Identity Test: Distinguishing Persistent Cognitive Identity from Persona Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22195)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"情境化身份测试"(SIT)框架，用于判定语言智能体是否真正"拥有"其所扮演人格的特定发展谱系，并证明仅基于压缩公共画像的条件策略在身份碰撞场景下平均情境有效性上限为1/m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能逼真地扮演人格、回忆对话并编织自传，但这种对话流畅性掩盖了一个根本的归因问题：表面像某个人不等于真正经历过其人生。两个个体可能共享相同的公共画像（年龄、故乡、职业、性格），却拥有完全不同的私人历史、关系和习得技能。仅以该共享画像为条件时，智能体缺乏判定哪条"谱系"为真所需的信息。作者旨在解决这一身份归因的根本缺陷。

Method: 提出架构无关的SIT评估框架：真实"接地身份"要求智能体既对已记录经历具备恰当知识，又对未接地经历具备恰当的"无知"，其边界由该身份实际习得的内容而非底层基础模型所知内容决定。作者证明：任何仅以压缩画像为条件的策略，在m条碰撞生活史、且查询具有谱系区分性时，平均情境有效性至多1/m（配对谱系下至多50%）。据此构建了SITBench评测套件，覆盖25个画像碰撞对（50条不同谱系）、10,000个规划探针和9种架构配置，并配套开源参考实现、确定性测试夹具及前沿模型（GPT-5.6 Sol与Claude Opus 5）的实证试点评估。

Result: 形式化地界定了画像碰撞条件下人格提示的失效模式：仅依赖公共画像无法在碰撞谱系间正确归因，平均情境有效性上限为1/m。SITBench为评估情境连续性、结构化状态和认知边界提供了保证性测试框架，并通过对前沿基础模型的试点评估初步验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 对话流畅性不等于真实的身份接地。SIT与SITBench为检验智能体行为是否可功能性地归因于特定发展谱系提供了理论界线和工程实现，揭示并量化了人格提示在画像碰撞下的根本局限，为后续围绕情境连续性、结构化状态与认知边界的保证机制奠定基础。

Abstract: Large language models can convincingly adopt personas, recall past dialogues, and weave rich autobiographies. Yet this conversational eloquence conceals a fundamental attribution problem: looking the part does not mean having lived the life. Two individuals can share identical public profiles--the same age, hometown, occupation, and personality traits--while possessing entirely distinct private histories, relationships, and acquired skills. When conditioned solely on that shared profile, an agent lacks the information required to determine which lineage is correct. We introduce the Situated Identity Test (SIT), an architecture-independent framework that evaluates whether an agent's behavior is functionally attributable to a specific developmental lineage. Grounded identity requires both appropriate knowledge of recorded experiences and appropriate ignorance of ungrounded ones, bounded by what the identity has actually acquired rather than what its underlying foundation model knows. We prove that any policy conditioned solely on a compressed profile is bounded by an average situated validity of at most 1/m across m colliding life histories on lineage-discriminative queries (at most 50% for paired lineages). We instantiate this framework in SITBench, an evaluation suite designed for 25 profile-collision pairs (50 distinct lineages) across 10,000 planned probes and nine architectural configurations. Supported by an open-source reference implementation, deterministic test fixtures, and empirical pilot evaluations on frontier foundation models (GPT-5.6 Sol and Claude Opus 5), we formalize the failure modes of persona prompting under profile collision and provide an assurance harness for evaluating episodic continuity, structured state, and epistemic boundaries.

</details>


### [89] [The Role of AI in Online Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22198)
*Valeria Lerman,Oren Rigbi,Yaniv Dover*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种经验方法，利用LLM的供给冲击（模型价格与能力的突变）并对比已验证与未验证评论，来识别生成式AI对在线平台内容的影响。对Trustpilot上1300多万条评论的分析显示，LLM供给冲击后未验证评论变得更负面（更多1星、更少5星），主要由新模型发布驱动，并集中于评论量最低和最高的企业，同时出现短促集中的评论爆发，表明生成式AI正在重塑平台声誉与竞争格局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的快速普及为在线平台上的战略性内容生成（包括可能有害的操纵行为）创造了新机会，可能削弱平台有效性并重塑平台动态。然而，由于AI生成内容很少能被直接观测，测量这类活动十分困难，亟需一种不依赖直接观测AI内容的间接识别方法。

Method: 提出一种经验识别策略：利用离散的LLM供给冲击（模型价格与能力的突变）作为外生变化来源，并对比已验证（verified）与未验证（non-verified）评论，识别与生成式AI供给改善相关的平台活动变化。该方法应用于Trustpilot平台上超过1300万条评论。

Result: 研究发现：LLM供给冲击后，未验证评论呈现更强的负面倾向——1星评论增多、5星评论减少、评分整体下降；该效应主要由新模型发布驱动，并集中在评论量最低和最高的企业；同时，LLM供给冲击会引发短促而集中的评论活动爆发。

Conclusion: 这些发现共同表明，生成式AI已经在重塑在线平台上声誉运作与竞争的方式，战略性的AI使用可能改变平台竞争动态。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of large language models (LLMs) creates new opportunities for strategic content generation on online platforms, including potentially harmful forms of manipulation that may undermine platform effectiveness and reshape platform dynamics. However, measuring such activity is difficult because AI-generated content is rarely directly observable.
