### [123] [ProcessLight: Process Supervision for Large Language Model Based Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22746)
*Huaitao Zhao,Tianlong Zhou,Weijie Wang,Jiasheng Shi,Weixiong Rao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ProcessLight 框架将交通信号控制决策分解为可验证的语义步骤，并基于此开发 STeP-PO 强化学习方法，通过步骤级信用分配优化推理过程，超越了仅依赖最终结果奖励的现有 LLM TSC 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的交通信号控制方法仅从最终结果进行优化，无法区分有效与错误的推理步骤，导致有用和误导性的步骤被一同更新，削弱了模型学习有效推理的能力。

Method: 提出 ProcessLight 框架把信号决策分解为可验证的语义步骤；在其上开发 Step-wise Traffic Process Policy Optimization (STeP-PO) 强化学习框架，利用步骤质量分数评估局部推理质量、步骤重要性衡量各步骤对最终动作的影响，在语义步骤树上分配步骤级优势，并将优势传播到推理 token，实现比仅结果奖励更细粒度的策略优化。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，所提方法显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过步骤级信用分配优化结构化推理过程，ProcessLight 与 STeP-PO 有效提升了基于 LLM 的交通信号控制性能，证明了细粒度过程优化优于单纯的结果优化。代码已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently been introduced into traffic signal control (TSC) as decision agents due to their strengths in human-readable reasoning generation. Yet, existing LLM TSC methods optimize only from final outcomes and fail to distinguish valid from flawed reasoning steps, causing useful or misleading steps to be jointly updated and thus impairing the model's learning of effective reasoning. To bridge this gap, we propose an LLM-based framework ProcessLight to decompose signal decisions into verifiable semantic steps. Building on ProcessLight, we further develop Step-wise Traffic Process Policy Optimization (STeP-PO), a novel reinforcement learning framework that optimizes structured reasoning processes through step-level credit assignment. Specifically, STeP-PO uses step quality scores to evaluate local reasoning quality and step importance to measure each step's influence on the final action, and then assigns step-level advantages over a semantic step tree structure. The resulting step-level advantages are propagated to reasoning tokens, enabling fine-grained policy optimization beyond outcome-only rewards. Extensive experiments over multiple real-world datasets demonstrate the superiority of our methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/wenzhaoabc/processlight.

</details>


### [124] [CTSpinoPelvic1K: spine, pelvis, ribs and femora in one coordinate frame, annotated for lumbosacral transitional anatomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22760)
*Gregory Schwing,Ashley Schehr,Annika Tekumulla,Margret Khoushi,Ryan Christian,Dane Hubers,Faris Mahjoub,Hassan Saad,Mia Sooch,Sathyagopal Siddapureddy,Michael McLellan,Jerick Kim,Miraziz Ismoilov,Nizar Alnabahneh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发布 CTSpinoPelvic1K 数据集：将 CTSpine1K 的椎体与 CTPelvic1K 的骨盆标签在同一 CT 序列上配对，并补充两者都缺失的骨骼（肋骨、股骨），以研究在不依赖全脊柱 C2 计数的情况下，仅凭局部形态学能否解决腰骶移行椎（LSTV）导致的椎体命名歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腰骶交界处的椎体命名依赖从 C2 向下计数（全脊柱影像），但临床腰椎手术计划常仅有 T12–S1 的腰椎局部影像，缺失 C2。当存在腰骶移行椎（LSTV）改变计数时，局部解剖产生歧义：四个无肋骨椎体既可能是带腰肋的 L1，也可能是骶化的 L5；六个无肋骨椎体可能是第六腰椎、肋骨发育不全的 T12 或腰化的 S1。作者希望探究局部形态学能否在不依赖计数的情况下解决这一歧义。

Method: 将 CTSpine1K 的椎体标签与 CTPelvic1K 的骨盆标签在每位患者骨骼最丰富的同一 CT 序列上配对，采用 VerSe 原生标注方案，并补充两者均未覆盖的肋骨与股骨。最终提供 802 例 CT，含逐椎体等级的肋骨与股骨标注；等级锚定于最下方肋椎与 S1；并提供 L6、T13、独立 S1 与腰肋等类别，使解剖异常得以如实记录。验证包括几何不变量（802/802 通过）、5749 根肋骨的肋-椎对应发生率（0.035% 偏移），以及脊柱骨盆指标与已发表数值的对照。

Result: 数据集共 802 例 CT 记录，提供 NIfTI 影像/标签对，按患者分组并做 LSTV 分层的五折划分及数据加载器；存档于 Zenodo（DOI: 10.5281/zenodo.22139642）。验证显示全部 802 例通过几何不变量检查，肋椎对应偏移率仅 0.035%，脊柱骨盆测量值与文献报道一致。

Conclusion: CTSpinoPelvic1K 将椎体与骨盆统一于同一序列并补充肋骨与股骨标注，为基于局部特征的椎体分类、尸体形态测量更新、脊柱骨盆评估、机会性筛查及术前手术规划研究（含 377 例俯卧位记录，填补公开术前腰椎队列空白）提供数据基础。局限包括：胸椎真值受扫描视野限制、体位角为仰卧位、无留出测试集、肋骨为分诊复核伪标签、Castellvi 分级仅 33 例为双读一致。

Abstract: Purpose: A vertebra at the lumbosacral junction is named by counting caudally from C2 on whole-spine imaging, but a lumbar case is planned on lumbar-only imaging (T12 to S1), without C2. Abdominopelvic CT holds that span plus the lowest ribs and pelvis. Where a lumbosacral transitional vertebra (LSTV) alters the count, the local anatomy is ambiguous: four rib-free vertebrae may be an L1 with a lumbar rib or an L5 assimilated to the sacrum, and six may be a sixth lumbar vertebra, a T12 with aplastic ribs, or a lumbarized S1. CTSpinoPelvic1K asks whether local morphology resolves it without the count. CTSpine1K's vertebrae and CTPelvic1K's pelvis covered these patients but were never joined; this release joins them on one series and adds the bones neither had. It provides 802 CT records with per-level ribs and femora, levels anchored on the lowest rib-bearing vertebra and S1, and classes for L6, T13, a separate S1 and lumbar ribs, so anomalies are recorded as such.
  Acquisition and Validation Methods: Records pair CTSpine1K and CTPelvic1K labels on each patient's bone-richest series under a VerSe-native scheme. Validation covered geometric invariants (802/802 pass), rib-vertebra incidence across 5,749 ribs (0.035% offset), and spinopelvic measures matching published values.
  Data Format and Usage Notes: NIfTI image/label pairs with patient-grouped LSTV-stratified five-fold splits and a loader; archived at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22139642.
  Potential Applications: Classifying a vertebra from local features; updating cadaveric morphometry; spinopelvic assessment; opportunistic screening; and, absent a public preoperative lumbar cohort, surgical planning research (377 records prone). Limitations: thoracic ground truth is field-of-view limited; postural angles supine; no held-out test set; ribs are triaged-review pseudolabels; Castellvi grades two-reader consensus on 33 records.

</details>


### [125] [DVA-Neurons: Design and Verification of Adaptive LIF Neurons: From Single-Neuron Dynamics to Multi-Neuron Spiking Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22775)
*Thanh Pham,Riadul Islam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展、验证并物理实现自适应 LIF 神经元，解决了 SNN 中神经元缺乏生物适应机制以及多神经元网络扩展时的突触权重分布与脉冲路由两大问题，在 SAED 14nm CMOS 工艺下完成了三种架构规模的实现与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN 有望实现超低功耗推理，但存在两大部署障碍：一是固定参数的 LIF 神经元缺乏生物神经元基于放电历史调节兴奋性的适应机制；二是从单神经元扩展到多神经元网络时，面临突触权重分布和神经元间脉冲路由等新挑战。

Method: 提出并实现三种架构规模的自适应 LIF 神经元：1) 带两级突触滤波的 2 阶神经元，提供更丰富的时序动态；2) 具有可配置权重（100 到 5）的全连接 6 神经元脉冲网络，演示基于权重的神经元间通信；3) 支持逐周期观测所有内部状态的直接验证方法。所有设计面向 SAED 14nm CMOS 工艺、1 GHz 频率综合，并使用基于 Cocotb 的 Python 测试平台在脉冲电流刺激（幅值 80、ISI=3）下验证。

Result: 适应机制有效调节放电：2 阶神经元放电抑制 31%（25 对比 36 个脉冲），网络中突触后放电减少 31%（18 对比 26 个脉冲）。物理开销方面，2 阶神经元比 1 阶基线多 1.77 倍面积和 1.52 倍功耗；6 神经元网络呈现近线性扩展（面积 5.7 倍、功耗 5.3 倍）。记录了 7 个验证缺陷，涵盖测试平台连接、定点溢出和 Verilog 表达式位宽语义。

Conclusion: 自适应 LIF 神经元在三种架构规模下均成功实现并验证，证明了适应机制对放电调节的有效性与多神经元网络近线性扩展的可行性，同时揭示了硬件实现与验证中的关键工程问题。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer a promising path toward ultra-low-power artificial intelligence inference by emulating the event-driven computation of biological neurons. However, two challenges limit their practical deployment. First, fixed-parameter Leaky Integrate-and-Fire (LIF) neurons lack the adaptation mechanisms observed in biology, where neurons modulate their excitability based on firing history. Second, scaling from single neurons to multi-neuron networks introduces challenges in synaptic weight distribution and inter-neuron spike routing that are absent in isolated designs. This paper addresses both issues through the extension, verification, and physical implementation of adaptive LIF neurons at three architectural scales. This work contributes: a 2nd-order neuron with two-stage synaptic filtering for richer temporal dynamics; a fully-connected 6-neuron spiking network with configurable weights (100 to 5) demonstrating weight-based inter-neuron communication; and a direct verification methodology enabling per-cycle observation of all internal states. All designs were synthesized targeting Selected Area Electron Diffraction (SAED) 14 nm Complementary Metal-Oxide-Semiconductor (CMOS) technology at 1 GHz and verified with Cocotb-based Python testbenches under pulsed current stimuli (amplitude 80, ISI=3). The results show that adaptation effectively modulates firing: 31% suppression in the 2nd-order neuron (25 vs.\ 36 spikes) and 31% reduction in postsynaptic firing in the network (18 vs.\ 26 spikes). Physically, the 2nd-order neuron costs 1.77x more area and 1.52x more power than the 1st-order baseline, while the 6-neuron network demonstrates near-linear scaling (5.7x area, 5.3x power). Seven verification bugs spanning testbench connectivity, fixed-point overflow, and Verilog expression-width semantics are documented.

</details>


### [126] [From Research Frontier to Laboratory Bench: Design of a Four-Tier Experimental Teaching System for Multimodal Medical Image Intelligent Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22790)
*Dongjing Shan,Yamei Luo,Jin Li,Yong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文设计了一套智能医学工程本科实验教学体系，将子宫内膜癌多模态深度学习研究转化为四层递进、含32个实验单元（64学时）的实验序列，并通过定制虚拟临床工作站与去标识化多机构数据实施，以弥合课程与临床AI真实复杂性之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能医学工程本科项目快速扩张，但实验课程滞后于临床AI的多模态、长尾分布与分布漂移等真实特性，存在模态、真实性与部署三大教育缺口，亟需将前沿多模态深度学习研究转化为结构化的本科实验教学。

Method: 将一项进行中的子宫内膜癌多模态深度学习研究转化为结构化本科实验序列；识别模态、真实性、部署三个教育缺口；从建构对齐、体验式学习、研究-教学纽带及CDIO框架中提炼四条教学原则；构建四层递进课程加工程层，含32个实验单元、64学时，通过定制虚拟临床工作站使用去标识化多机构数据交付；每一层对应特定技术瓶颈、先修课程与标准参照式成果；并规定数据治理、安全与评估协议，规划两个实施周期的学习成果数据收集。

Result: 形成一套可实施的多模态医学AI本科实验教学体系：四层递进课程加工程层、32个实验单元覆盖64学时，逐层映射到技术瓶颈、先修课程与标准参照式成果；配套虚拟临床工作站、去标识化多机构数据及数据治理/安全/评估协议，并设计了跨两个实施周期的学习成果数据收集方案。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一套基于真实多模态研究项目转化的高级实验教学系统，通过系统化课程层级、明确的教学原则与完善的治理评估机制，有望填补智能医学工程本科实验教学在模态、真实性与部署方面的空白，为后续实证评估奠定基础。

Abstract: Undergraduate programmes in intelligent medical engineering are expanding, yet laboratory curricula lag behind the multimodal, long-tailed, and distributionally shifting realities of clinical AI. This design paper presents an advanced experimental teaching system that translates an ongoing multimodal deep learning research project on endometrial carcinoma into a structured undergraduate lab sequence. We identify three educational gaps (modality, authenticity, and deployment) and derive four pedagogical principles from constructive alignment, experiential learning, the research teaching nexus, and the CDIO framework. The curriculum comprises four progressive tiers plus an engineering layer, with 32 laboratory units over 64 contact hours, delivered via a custom virtual clinical workstation using de-identified multi-institutional data. Each tier maps to a specific technical bottleneck, prerequisite coursework, and criterion-referenced deliverables. Data governance, safety, and assessment protocols are specified. Learning outcome data will be collected across two implementation cycles.

</details>


### [127] [ISA-Bench: A Benchmark for Computational Reasoning Across Instruction Set Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22878)
*Aditya Pola,Arkaprava Majumdar,Vineeth N. Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ISA-Bench，一个基于受限指令集编程游戏的代码生成基准，用以评估大语言模型在陌生计算模型下的推理能力，并通过推理-执行差距（REG）分析揭示"想到策略"与"写出正确程序"之间的断裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准主要面向 Python、Java 等训练数据充足的常用语言，无法有效衡量模型在陌生计算模型中的推理能力，例如从单条 subtract 指令推导算术、协调多节点并行程序、或把逻辑门连接成电路。

Method: 构建 ISA-Bench 基准，包含一组受限指令集的编程游戏；为每个游戏提供完整执行栈（解析器、虚拟机、验证器），实现自动化评测并支持结构化反馈以进行迭代改进；并提出推理-执行差距（REG）分析方法，对比模型识别可行计算策略与将其表达为正确目标 ISA 程序之间的落差。

Result: 推理型模型的平均解题率高于代码专用模型和通用模型；陌生语法仍是主要失败原因；引入迭代反馈后各模型解题任务增多，但增益在不同架构间差异显著；REG 分析揭示模型普遍存在"能想到合理策略却写不出正确程序"的反复脱节现象。

Conclusion: ISA-Bench 为评估模型在陌生指令集上的真实推理能力提供了更严格的测试环境和自动化反馈机制，REG 分析系统性定位了模型从策略构想到代码实现的瓶颈，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language model code generation benchmarks primarily evaluate well-resourced languages like Python and Java, where models benefit from abundant training data. They provide limited evidence about reasoning in unfamiliar computational models: deriving arithmetic from a single subtract instruction, coordinating parallel programs across communicating nodes, or wiring logic gates into circuits. We present ISA-Bench, a benchmark of programming games with constrained instruction sets. For each game we provide a full execution stack (parser, VM, and verifier), enabling automated evaluation with structured feedback for iterative refinement. Reasoning models achieve higher average solve rates than code-specialized and general-purpose models, but unfamiliar syntax remains a major source of failure. Models solve more tasks with iterative feedback, though the gains vary substantially across architectures. We introduce a reasoning--execution gap (REG) analysis that reveals a recurring disconnect between identifying a plausible computational strategy and expressing it as a correct program in the target ISA. Code is open-sourced.

</details>


### [128] [When Should a VLM Look? Paying Only for Visual Calls That Were Needed and Used](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22910)
*Kunyu Peng,Junming Liu,Ruiqi He,Qingzhuo Wang,Jianzhong Qi,Xianhui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CounterCredit 通过"现金返还+租金"的信用机制，对视觉智能体的每一次图像返回型工具调用同时验证其"必要性"与"证据使用性"，从而抑制虚假调用并提升定位准确率，在同等起点与预算下显著超越仅用结果奖励的 GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言智能体（可裁剪缩放图像）只用"结果奖励"来训练，但一次成功的工具调用并不意味着模型真的需要观察、或真的用到了返回的像素：冷启动检查点上仅 10%~12% 的视觉调用既必要又被使用，已发布智能体在单基准上虚假调用率高达 36%~87%。结果奖励、评判奖励与分支探测各自只能观察到问题的一个侧面，约三分之二的结果奖励被支付给了"既不需要也没使用"的调用。

Method: CounterCredit 对每一次返回图像的工具调用，在"执行前状态"用策略自身的金答案分数提出两个问题：①决策值——比较"执行视觉分支"与"立即回答"的结果差异，衡量该调用是否必要；②证据值——把返回的裁剪图替换为随机同尺寸补丁后再次执行同一调用并比较分数，衡量模型是否真正使用了像素证据。只有在两方面都通过验证的调用可获得"现金返还"（cashback），其余所有已执行调用都要付"租金"（rent）；价格有上界保证每条正确轨迹严格优于每条错误轨迹，并采用双通道 GRPO 优势使价格保持在其自身单位内。

Result: 在与结果奖励 GRPO 相同的冷启动点、提示池和预算下，CounterCredit 在 V* 达 89.5%、HR-Bench-4K 达 80.2%、HR-Bench-8K 达 76.4%，比单纯结果奖励 GRPO 高 6.3~9.4 个百分点，且每问题调用次数为 1.78 次（对比 1.84 次）。同时虚假调用率降至 31%~36%，为所有评估智能体中最低。同一方法将 Qwen3-VL-8B 基座的平均得分从 75.4 提升至 80.8。

Conclusion: CounterCredit 通过同时验证调用必要性（决策值）与证据使用性（证据值）的信用机制，有效消除了视觉智能体中大量虚假工具调用，在改善定位、计数等基准表现的同时降低了调用开销，证明对每一次视觉调用进行"双问"核算是引导工具使用行为更可靠、更高效的训练信号。

Abstract: Vision-language agents that crop and zoom are trained with rewards that credit a successful tool call, yet a successful call does not show that the model needed to look or used the pixels it received. On our cold-start checkpoint only 10% to 12% of visual calls were both needed and used, and released agents make spurious calls 36% to 87% of the time on individual benchmarks. Outcome rewards, judge rewards, and branch probes each observe one side of this failure, and about two thirds of what an outcome reward pays goes to calls that were neither needed nor used. CounterCredit asks both questions of every image-returning call at its realized pre-call state, using the policy's own gold-answer score. A decision value compares the realized visual branch with answering immediately; an evidence value compares the returned crop with random same-size patches substituted into the same call. A call verified on both earns cashback and every other executed call pays rent; the price is bounded so that every correct trajectory outranks every wrong one, and a dual-channel GRPO advantage keeps the price in its own units. From the same cold start, prompt pool, and budget, CounterCredit reaches 89.5% on V*, 80.2% on HR-Bench-4K, and 76.4% on HR-Bench-8K, 6.3 to 9.4 points above outcome-only GRPO at 1.78 against 1.84 calls per question, and lowers the spurious-call rate to 31% to 36%, the lowest among the agents evaluated. The same recipe lifts a Qwen3-VL-8B base from 75.4 to 80.8 on average.

</details>


### [129] [Beyond Linear Context: Graph-Guided Evidence Navigation for Long-Novel Reasoning with a Local 9B Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22939)
*Wenji Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文测试了在冻结知识图谱路由下，一个小型本地模型（qwen3.5:9b）在30部侦探小说、234道多选题上的长文本问答表现。最强图谱路由达到53.85%准确率，优于近期窗口(46.15%)、整书压缩(51.28%)、向量检索(51.71%)和仅问题基线(40.17%)，但所有图谱与基线对比均未通过Holm校正，故作为探索性设计证据呈现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文模型按叙事顺序逐token阅读，受固定注意力预算限制，难以让小说开头线索与结尾问题建立关联。作者借鉴侦探的推理方式（按时间梳理事件、维护人物关系图谱），探索冻结知识图谱能否赋予小型局部模型同样的跨章节关联自由度。

Method: 使用固定模型qwen3.5:9b作为阅读器，在30部侦探小说和234道多选题上，于九种条件下评测：五种图谱路由、近期窗口基线、整书压缩、普通向量检索、仅问题对照。构建两种图谱管线，评估其标注覆盖率和准确率，并对十五个图谱-基线对照做Holm校正。

Result: 最强图谱路由53.85%(126/234)，近期窗口46.15%，整书压缩51.28%，向量检索51.71%，仅问题40.17%。在无书无法作答的子集上图谱路由达42.86%。十五个对照均未通过Holm校正。两个结构性发现：标注线索证据集中于图谱拓扑核心（合并后2.35倍富集）；两条图谱管线线索段落标注覆盖率差异巨大（16%对73%），说明仅看合并准确率会掩盖所测瓶颈。

Conclusion: 图谱路由的准确率提升未通过多重比较校正，仅能作为探索性设计证据。但更稳健的发现是：证据在拓扑核心中富集，以及图谱管线间的覆盖率差异表明合并准确率会掩盖真实瓶颈所在。

Abstract: Long-context models read a novel the way a person reads a printout: one token after another, in narrative order, with the whole history competing for a fixed budget of attention. A detective does not work that way. They sort what happened when, and they keep a map of who relates to whom, so a clue from chapter one can meet a question asked at the end of the book. We test whether a frozen knowledge graph can give a small local model that same freedom. Thirty detective novels and 234 multiple-choice questions are answered by one fixed qwen3.5:9b reader under nine conditions: five graph routes, a recent-window baseline, whole-book compression, ordinary vector retrieval, and a question-only control. The strongest graph route reaches 53.85% (126/234) against 46.15% for the recent window, 51.28% for compression, 51.71% for vector retrieval and 40.17% for question-only. On the subset that no model can answer without the book, the graph route reaches 42.86%. None of the fifteen graph-baseline contrasts survives Holm correction, so we present the result as exploratory evidence about a design. Two structural findings survive scrutiny better than the headline number: annotated evidence concentrates in the topological core of these graphs (2.35x enrichment, pooled), and the two graph-building pipelines differ so much in annotation coverage (16% versus 73% of clue paragraphs) that pooled accuracy alone would hide which bottleneck is being measured.

</details>


### [130] [AgentRouter: Heterogeneous Model Routing for Cost-Optimal Multi-Step Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22951)
*Rudrendu Kumar Paul,Sourav Nandy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentRouter，一个 12M 参数的轻量分类器，在智能体轨迹的每一步动态选择四个模型层级之一，从而在保持 97.3% 端到端任务完成质量的同时，实现相对全前沿模型 72% 的成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级智能体系统若将轨迹中每一步都路由到前沿模型，会浪费 60–80% 的推理预算，因为这些子任务其实可以由更小的模型同样胜任。现有路由方案只优化单轮查询分配，忽略了智能体工作流的关键特性：在单条轨迹内部，子任务的复杂度差异巨大——规划步骤需要前沿级推理，而后续格式化步骤只需 7B 模型即可。

Method: 将步级模型路由形式化为智能体轨迹上的顺序分配问题，并提出 AgentRouter：一个 12M 参数的轻量分类器（A100 GPU 上每步开销 <5ms），利用路由时即可提取的五个特征，将每一步映射到四个模型层级之一。模型在 50,000 条标注智能体轨迹步骤上训练，覆盖规划、编码、研究和数据分析任务。

Result: AgentRouter 相对仅用前沿模型的基线实现 72% 的成本降低，并保留 97.3% 的前沿模型质量（端到端任务完成退化不足 3%）。步级路由准确率在最低复杂度步骤上达 91%，在高效层级步骤上达 85%，在较难的中档和前沿层级上为 76–82%。在相同基准上，逐步骤应用的 RouteLLM 和 FrugalGPT 仅分别实现 31% 和 44% 的成本降低，因为其单轮训练信号缺失了轨迹级质量依赖。

Conclusion: 通过在智能体轨迹的步骤粒度上进行模型路由并显式建模轨迹级质量依赖，AgentRouter 能在大幅压缩推理成本的同时保持近乎不变的任务完成质量，显著优于仅针对单轮查询优化的现有路由方法。

Abstract: Enterprise agentic systems that route every trajectory step to a frontier model waste 60-80% of their inference budget on subtasks that smaller models handle equally well. Existing routing solutions optimize single-turn query assignment but ignore a property unique to agentic workflows: subtask complexity varies widely within a single trajectory. A planning step may require frontier-class reasoning while a subsequent formatting step needs only a 7B model. We formalize step-level model routing as a sequential assignment problem over agent trajectories and propose AgentRouter, a lightweight classifier (12M parameters, <5ms overhead per step on an A100 GPU) that maps each trajectory step to one of four model tiers using five features extractable at routing time. Trained on 50,000 annotated agent trajectory steps spanning planning, coding, research, and data analysis tasks, AgentRouter achieves 72% cost reduction relative to frontier-only baselines, retaining 97.3% of frontier-only quality (less than 3% degradation in end-to-end task completion); per-step routing accuracy reaches 91% on minimal-complexity steps and 85% on efficient-tier steps, with 76-82% on the harder mid-range and frontier tiers. On the same benchmarks, RouteLLM and FrugalGPT (applied per-step) achieve only 31% and 44% cost reduction respectively, because their single-turn training signal misses trajectory-level quality dependencies.

</details>


### [131] [A Compact Stance-Indexed Anterior-Posterior COP Representation for Parkinson's Disease Classification from Plantar VGRF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22956)
*Md. Sifat,Sania Akter,Akif Islam,Md. Ekramul Hamid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出 AP-COP10 这一紧凑表示，在归一化站立相固定位置保留前后向压力中心信息及双侧不对称性，在帕金森病步态分类中 AUC 达 0.894，优于三种文献衍生的 COP 表示，并证明其携带难以被更广泛工程步态描述符恢复的判别信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病会改变步态和双侧协调性，而机器学习性能同时也取决于连续步态信号的表示方式。研究旨在探究在归一化站立相固定位置保留前后向压力中心（AP-COP）信息，是否能够作为足底压力步态信号的一种紧凑且富含信息的表示。

Method: 使用帕金森病步态数据库（Gait in Parkinson's Disease Database）中 165 名受试者的双侧垂直地面反作用力记录，采用重复的全嵌套参与者级交叉验证进行评估。提出 AP-COP10 表示，即跨五个站立相窗口的 AP-COP 位置与双侧不对称性指标。将其与三种经协调处理的文献衍生 COP 表示进行比较；并考察互补的 25 个非 AP-COP 描述符、完整的 35 特征表示，以及特征竞争、来源研究留出验证和传感器扰动分析。

Result: AP-COP10 的 AUC 为 0.894，在同一评估流程下优于三种文献衍生的 COP 表示。单独的 25 个非 AP-COP 描述符 AUC 为 0.856，完整 35 特征表示 AUC 为 0.908。从完整表示中移除 AP-COP10 导致判别性能出现统计学支持的显著下降，而将互补描述符加入 AP-COP10 仅带来微小的、无统计学支持的提升。特征竞争显示，最具判别力的站立相索引描述符集中在站立早期和早期-中期；来源研究留出与传感器扰动分析支持了该表示的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 站立相索引的 AP-COP 保留了更广泛工程步态描述符难以恢复的判别信息，支持采用紧凑且可解释的表示来进行病理性步态的机器学习分析。

Abstract: Parkinson's disease alters gait and bilateral coordination, but machine-learning performance also depends on how continuous gait signals are represented. This study investigates whether preserving anterior-posterior center-of-pressure (AP-COP) information at fixed locations across normalized stance provides a compact and informative representation of plantar-force gait signals. Bilateral vertical ground reaction force recordings from 165 participants in the Gait in Parkinson's Disease Database were evaluated using repeated fully nested participant-level cross-validation. We propose AP-COP10, comprising AP-COP position and bilateral asymmetry across five stance windows. AP-COP10 achieved an AUC of 0.894 and outperformed three harmonized literature-derived COP representations under the same evaluation pipeline. The complementary 25 non-AP-COP descriptors alone achieved an AUC of 0.856, while the complete 35-feature representation achieved 0.908. Removing AP-COP10 from the complete representation produced a statistically supported loss in discrimination, whereas adding the complementary descriptors to AP-COP10 yielded only a small, unsupported improvement. Feature competition indicated that the most informative stance-indexed descriptors were concentrated in early and early-mid stance, while source-study holdout and sensor-perturbation analyses supported the robustness of the representation. These findings indicate that stance-indexed AP-COP retains discriminative information that is not readily recovered by broader engineered gait descriptors, supporting compact and interpretable representations for machine-learning analysis of pathological gait.

</details>


### [132] [R-GEAN: Regimen-Guided Edit Action Network for Within-Admission Medication Change Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22959)
*Regan Mahat,Mansu Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究住院期间药物方案的变更预测，提出防泄漏基准用于预测入院24小时至出院之间ATC3药物类别的净增与净减，并引入非对称候选评分网络R-GEAN，其在编辑综合指标上优于RETAIN、GAMENet、MICRON等基线，同时揭示完整方案评估与编辑级评估衡量的是不同维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住院期间医生会不断启停、继续药物，但预测完整出院方案的传统评估指标会奖励模型照抄未变化的药物，即使模型没有识别出任何实际变更，导致评估失真。因此需要专门针对"药物变更"这一实际临床决策建立无泄漏的评估基准。

Method: 构建防泄漏基准：仅使用既往完整住院记录和当前入院前24小时信息，预测ATC3级别的净增（24小时未活跃的类别）与净减（24小时活跃的类别）；提出R-GEAN非对称候选评分网络，分别设置独立的增加预测器和移除预测器；采用编辑综合指标（edit composite，汇总增加、移除、方案变更和动作模式四类表现）进行评估，并以micro-F1和Spearman相关分析与完整方案评估的关系。

Result: 在82,286名患者、240,480次入院数据上，R-GEAN取得最高编辑综合指标0.464，优于最强主比较基线0.435；复现的RETAIN、GAMENet、MICRON分别为0.428、0.420、0.288。R-GEAN优势集中在正确识别出院时已不再活跃的药物类别，而罕见新增及多药变更的入院仍较困难。编辑综合指标与基于重建出院方案的micro-F1排序相关性弱（Spearman r=0.20）；单纯延续基线在不预测任何增删的情况下反而取得最高的完整方案得分。

Conclusion: 完整方案评估与编辑级评估衡量的是药物预测的不同方面，不能混用；该基准评估的是实际处方变更行为而非治疗合理性。R-GEAN通过独立处理增删，在识别药物停止方面表现突出。

Abstract: The medications prescribed to a patient often change during a hospital admission as clinicians start, stop, or continue therapies. We study whether models can predict which medication classes are added or removed between 24 hours after admission and discharge. Metrics that compare the complete discharge regimen can reward models for copying medications that remain unchanged, even when they identify no actual changes. We therefore introduce a leakage-controlled benchmark that predicts net ATC3 additions and removals using only prior completed admissions and information available within the first 24 hours of the current admission. Addition candidates are classes not active at 24 hours, whereas removal candidates are classes active at that time. We also introduce R-GEAN, an asymmetric candidate-scoring network with independent addition and removal predictors. Across 240,480 admissions from 82,286 patients, R-GEAN achieves the highest predefined summary of addition, removal, changed-regimen, and action-pattern performance, termed the edit composite (0.464), compared with 0.435 for the strongest primary comparator. Reimplemented RETAIN, GAMENet, and MICRON baselines obtain 0.428, 0.420, and 0.288, respectively. R-GEAN's advantage is concentrated in correctly identifying medication classes no longer active at discharge, while rare additions and admissions with multiple medication changes remain difficult. Rankings based on micro-F1 over the reconstructed discharge regimen and the edit composite correlate weakly across the evaluated models (Spearman r = 0.20). The continuation baseline achieves the highest complete-regimen score despite predicting no additions or removals. These results show that complete-regimen and edit-level evaluation measure different aspects of medication prediction. The benchmark evaluates observed prescribing changes, not treatment appropriateness

</details>


### [133] [OptiSkill: A Hierarchical and Evolving SkillBank for LLM-Based Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22987)
*Ruiqing Zhao,Rui Liu,Yuan Zuo,Huarong Zhang,Xiao Han,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OptiSkill 是一个面向 LLM 运筹学建模的技能增强框架，通过构建分层且可演化的 SkillBank（全局策略 + 步骤经验）存储求解器验证过的可复用技能，并借助批量级测试时演化进行稳健更新，从而持续提升数学规划建模的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化运筹学建模方法往往孤立地求解问题，几乎不保留可复用经验，导致重复出现相似的公式化错误；而此前的记忆型方法只存储示例、想法或洞见作为参考，缺乏能够跨问题叙事迁移并指导具体建模决策的“可复用公式化技能”，难以满足 OR 建模的需求。

Method: 提出 OptiSkill 框架，构建分层且不断演化的 SkillBank：其中 Global Strategies 提供问题级公式化骨架，Step Experiences 提供局部防错规则，所有经验均经求解器验证后存为可复用技能；并通过稳定的批量级测试时演化（batch-level test-time evolution）对候选技能先验证再纳入，确保技能库的可靠性。

Result: 在八个 OR 建模基准上的实验表明，OptiSkill 在不同 LLM 骨干模型上均提升了公式化准确率，优于强力的智能体基线方法，并且通过扩大 SkillBank 的覆盖范围与可靠性还能获得进一步提升。

Conclusion: OptiSkill 证明以分层、可演化技能库形式存储求解器验证过的经验，能有效让 LLM 跨问题复用公式化技能、减少重复错误并持续改进，为自动化 OR 建模提供了一条更高效、可积累的路径。

Abstract: Automated operations research (OR) modeling requires LLMs to translate natural-language decision problems into correct mathematical programs. Existing methods can improve individual formulations, but they often solve problems in isolation, retaining little reusable experience and repeating similar formulation errors. Prior memory-based approaches store examples, thoughts, or insights as references, while OR modeling requires reusable formulation skills that transfer across problem narratives and guide concrete modeling decisions. We propose OptiSkill, a skill-augmented framework that builds a hierarchical and evolving SkillBank for LLM-based OR modeling. SkillBank stores solver-verified experience as reusable skills, with Global Strategies for problem-level formulation skeletons and Step Experiences for local error-prevention rules. It is further refined through stable batch-level test-time evolution, where candidate skills are incorporated only after validation. Experiments on eight OR modeling benchmarks show that OptiSkill improves formulation accuracy across LLM backbones, outperforms strong agentic baselines, and gains further by expanding SkillBank coverage and reliability. Code and data are available at https://github.com/rachhhhing/OptiSkill

</details>


### [134] [PINNForge: Execution-Grounded Evolutionary Design of Physics-Informed Neural Networks for PDE Solving via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23023)
*Mingyang Yu,Xu Yang,Jun Zhang,Xiaolong Wang,Jing Xu,Keqian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PINNsForge 是一个由 LLM 驱动的进化式框架，通过“生成—训练执行—反馈—进化”的闭环自动化设计 PINN 配置，在 25 个 PDE 基准中 24 个任务上取得最低平均 MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN 的性能高度依赖网络表示、采样、损失构建与优化等配置的协调选择，而这些有效配置随 PDE 的不同差异很大。现有自动化设计方法虽能搜索候选配置，但训练中揭示的信息仅用于评估而非改进后续设计，导致反复试错与训练预算浪费。

Method: 提出 PINNsForge——一个基于执行反馈、由 LLM 驱动的进化框架。它从 PDE 相关先验知识生成多样化候选配置，通过实际训练评估这些配置，并将高性能设计连同累积的执行证据反馈给 LLM；LLM 依据观察到的优化行为对耦合的 PINN 设计组件进行精化、重组与探索，形成生成、执行、反馈、进化的持续循环。

Result: 在 25 个 PDE 基准上，PINNsForge 在 24 个任务上取得最低平均 MSE，优于 RoPINN、PINNsFormer 和 PINNsAgent。消融实验表明，移除 PDE 知识库、执行反馈和进化搜索后，平均 MSE 分别升至完整 PINNsForge 的 3.74 倍、12.10 倍和 10.10 倍。

Conclusion: 不同于一次性搜索或仅用于评估的反馈，PINNsForge 能将训练经验逐步转化为针对目标 PDE 的改进设计决策，显著提升 PINN 自动化设计的效率与效果。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) require coordinated choices over network representation, sampling, loss construction, and optimization, while effective configurations often vary substantially across partial differential equations (PDEs). Existing automated PINN design methods can search candidate configurations, but information revealed during actual training is still used mainly for evaluation rather than to improve subsequent design, leading to repeated trial-and-error and inefficient use of training budget. We propose PINNsForge, an LLM-driven evolutionary framework for execution-feedback-based automated PINN design. PINNsForge generates diverse candidate configurations from PDE-related prior knowledge, evaluates them through actual training, and feeds high-performing designs together with accumulated execution evidence back to the LLM. Guided by observed optimization behavior, the LLM then refines, recombines, and explores coupled PINN design components, forming a continual cycle of generation, execution, feedback, and evolution. Unlike one-shot search or evaluation-only feedback, PINNsForge progressively converts training experience into improved design decisions for the target PDE. Across 25 PDE benchmarks, PINNsForge achieves the lowest mean MSE on 24 tasks compared with RoPINN, PINNsFormer, and PINNsAgent. Ablation studies further confirm the importance of the PDE knowledge base, execution feedback, and evolutionary search: removing these components increases the mean MSE to 3.74$\times$, 12.10$\times$, and 10.10$\times$ that of the full PINNsForge, respectively.

</details>


### [135] [Spatial-Interactor: Learning Spatial Reasoning through Interaction with the Observable Physical World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23038)
*Kaixiang Yao,Xu Wang,Miao Pan,Hu Xiyue,Weishi Wang,Daniel Dahlmeier,Jintao Chen,Yongliang Shen,Xuhong Zhang,Wenqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Spatial-Interactor 框架与 LSI-108K 数据集，通过三级课程（L1 被动世界状态转移、L2 主动自身状态转移、L3 长程交互轨迹）训练 VLM 建模物理世界状态转移，并采用 SFT + On-Policy Distillation 两阶段策略提升局部转移建模与长程状态整合能力，在多个基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）在动态环境中的空间推理能力不足，难以感知由物体运动和视角变化引起的局部状态转移，也难以沿长轨迹整合这些转移以维持更新的空间状态。现有空间训练主要针对静态问题（物体属性与空间关系），对状态转移缺乏直接监督；而交互轨迹天然连接了前一观察、动作与后一观察，可提供对局部状态转移的直接监督，完整轨迹还能揭示连续转移之间的依赖关系。

Method: 提出 Spatial-Interactor 框架，将学习过程组织为三级课程：L1 被动世界状态转移、L2 主动自身状态转移、L3 长程交互轨迹；并构建源自模拟与真实交互轨迹的 LSI-108K 数据集，任务与各级目标对齐。训练采用两阶段策略：第一阶段用 SFT 在 L1、L2 上进行局部转移建模；第二阶段用 On-Policy Distillation（OPD）进行特权自蒸馏，即教师分支获取片段级转移描述，监督学生的 on-policy 思维链（CoT），帮助学生在 L3 长轨迹上学习整合连续转移。

Result: 在多个 VLM 和空间推理基准上的实验表明，该方法在局部状态转移建模和长程状态整合两方面均取得一致性的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过以交互轨迹为核心的三级课程与两阶段训练（SFT + 特权自蒸馏 OPD），Spatial-Interactor 有效增强了 VLM 对动态环境空间状态转移的感知与长程整合能力，为面向物理世界的动态空间推理提供了新的训练范式。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is essential for vision-language models (VLMs) to understand and act in the physical world. Reasoning in dynamic environments requires VLMs to perceive local state transitions caused by object motion and viewpoint changes and integrate them over long trajectories to maintain an updated spatial state, yet existing VLMs remain limited in both capabilities. Current spatial training primarily focuses on static questions about object attributes and spatial relations, providing limited direct supervision for state transitions; in contrast, interaction trajectories naturally connect a preceding observation, an action, and a subsequent observation, offering direct supervision for local state transitions, while complete trajectories reveal dependencies among consecutive transitions. We therefore introduce Spatial-Interactor, a framework that trains VLMs to model physical-world state transitions through interaction, organizing this learning process into a three-level curriculum covering L1 passive world-state transitions, L2 active self-state transitions, and L3 long-horizon interaction trajectories. Accordingly, we construct the Learning from Spatial Interaction dataset (LSI-108K) from simulated and real interaction trajectories, with tasks aligned with the objective of each level. Our two-stage training strategy applies Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) to L1 and L2 for local transition modeling, and On-Policy Distillation (OPD) then uses privileged self-distillation: a teacher branch given segment-level transition descriptions supervises the student's on-policy CoT, helping the student learn to integrate consecutive transitions over L3 long trajectories. Experiments across multiple VLMs and spatial benchmarks show consistent gains in local transition modeling and long-horizon integration.

</details>


### [136] [Enforcing Narrative Reliability and Epistemic Pacing in LLM-Driven Detective Games via Structured Knowledge Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23043)
*Parsa Rahmati,Richard Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"结构化知识树 + 三智能体LLM流水线"架构，用于侦探审讯游戏的对话控制，将知识检索、对话生成与响应验证分离，确保虚拟嫌疑人只透露当前叙事状态允许的信息。实验表明该架构将关键幻觉降低64.78%，并完全杜绝了叙事信息的过早泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM使互动游戏能进行开放式对话，但其非确定性输出难以保持作者控制、事实一致性与预设的信息披露顺序。这在侦探游戏中尤为致命——过早揭示或虚构细节会破坏玩家的推理进展逻辑。

Method: 设计结构化知识树（Structured Knowledge Tree）架构，并配以三智能体（tri-agent）LLM流水线，将知识检索、对话生成、响应验证三个环节分离，确保虚拟嫌疑人仅揭示当前叙事状态所允许的信息。通过可玩的侦探游戏测试床《The Interrogation of Adrian Gale》和正式用户研究进行评估，考察幻觉减少、对预设披露序列的遵循度及玩家的逻辑进展感知。

Result: 结构化架构将关键幻觉降低64.78%，并完全防止了叙事信息的过早披露；但其严格的机械约束在强制对话揭示方面带来了一定可用性代价。

Conclusion: 该系统成功实现了严格的认知节奏（epistemic pacing）控制，并为玩家提供了清晰、主观的破案进展感，尽管在强制对话揭示上存在可用性权衡。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) enable open-ended dialogue in interactive games, but their non-deterministic outputs make it difficult to preserve authorial control, factual consistency, and the intended sequence of information disclosure. These challenges are particularly significant in detective games, where premature revelation or fabricated details can undermine the logic of player progression. We present a Structured Knowledge Tree architecture coupled with a tri-agent LLM pipeline for controlling dialogue in an open-ended interrogation game. The system separates knowledge retrieval, dialogue generation, and response verification to ensure that the virtual suspect reveals only information permitted by the current narrative state. We evaluate the approach through The Interrogation of Adrian Gale, a playable detective-game testbed, and a formal user study examining hallucination reduction, adherence to authored disclosure sequences, and perceived logical progression. Our results demonstrate that the structured architecture reduces critical hallucinations by 64.78% and entirely prevents premature narrative disclosure. While the strict mechanical constraints introduced usability trade-offs regarding forced conversational reveals, the system successfully enforces rigorous epistemic pacing and provides players with a clear, subjective sense of progression toward solving the case.

</details>


### [137] [LazyAgent: Demand-Driven Materialization and Physical Optimization of Agentic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23058)
*Xin Heng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LazyAgent 提出一种面向智能体自编程序的统一惰性执行框架，基于目标驱动的需求集合，仅在目标实际需要时才物化就绪节点，从而在请求范围内避免无关计算，带来显著成本节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体运行时普遍采用"规划后立即执行"的急切（eager）策略：一旦某步骤就绪就执行，导致程序即使描述得比当前请求更宽泛，也会执行大量与本次请求无关的工作。作者希望将执行与"请求所需"挂钩，避免请求范围之外的无关任务启动。

Method: LazyAgent 采用以"活的、由目标推导出的需求集合（demanded set）"为核心的统一执行框架。它从请求的输出出发维护后向闭包（backward closure），并在执行状态变化时更新该闭包；仅当活跃目标需要某个就绪节点时才将其物化。方案用一次线性时间的图分析加常数时间的成员测试替代反复的局部判断，使程序保持宽泛而执行保持请求特定。此外还提供了权限（permission）、目标相对输出投影（goal-relative output projection）、排序、复用与剪枝等机制。

Result: 在描述内容多于当前请求需要的程序上，LazyAgent 一致优于最强的"目标停止式"急切基线：加入一个无关产品使急切账单增加 22.5%，而 LazyAgent 为 0.0%；在科学生产工作流上节省 42.0% 的测量 CPU，在一个跨四仓库的实时发布门禁上节省 51.7% 的容器时间。当请求覆盖整个图时，证明并验证了与急切执行精确等价。目标相对输出投影在两个第三方测试套件上最多节省约 90% 的共享步骤开销，而相同急切控制节省 0.0%；当被省略输出无其他消费者或被请求需要时该优势消失。排序、复用与剪枝也能节省成本，但不能替代权限。此外，当前公开基准是"急切形状"的，几乎不含未请求的工作；预注册的规划干预也未使其扩大。

Conclusion: 按需惰性执行能系统性避免请求范围外的无关工作，带来可观且可验证的资源节省；现有基准缺少未请求工作，无法体现此类收益，应构建基于常驻程序（standing programs）与序列的基准以支撑该研究方向。

Abstract: Current agent runtimes that plan before acting generally execute a step once it becomes ready. We present LazyAgent, a unified execution framework for agent-authored programs organized around a live, goal-derived demanded set. LazyAgent refreshes a backward closure from requested outputs as execution state changes and materializes a ready node only when the active goal requires it. This replaces repeated local judgments with one linear-time graph analysis followed by constant-time membership tests, allowing programs to remain broad while execution stays request-specific. On programs that describe more than the current request needs, LazyAgent consistently outperforms the strongest goal-stopping eager baseline by refusing unrelated work before it starts. Adding one unrelated product raises the eager bill by 22.5% and LazyAgent's by 0.0%. LazyAgent saves 42.0% of measured CPU on production scientific workflows and 51.7% of container time on a live release gate spanning four repositories. We also prove and verify exact equivalence when the request reaches the whole graph, leaving no unrelated work to avoid. Beyond permission, goal-relative output projection saves up to approximately 90% of a shared step on two third-party test suites while the identical eager control saves 0.0%; the advantage disappears when the omitted output has no other consumer or the request needs it. Ordering, reuse, and pruning can also save cost, but do not replace permission. Finally, we show that current public benchmarks are eager-shaped and contain almost no unrequested work. A pre-registered planning intervention did not broaden them. These findings motivate benchmarks built from standing programs and sequences.

</details>


### [138] [FireWorldBench: Benchmarking Complex Physical World Intelligence through Coupled-Field Fire Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23064)
*Qiang Chen,Hao Guo,Huatai Zhu,Tairan Huang,Yichao Cao,Hongyan Xu,Keke Huang,Haifeng Li,Yi Chen,Xiu Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FireWorldBench 是一个通过耦合场火灾动力学评估多模态大语言模型"复杂物理世界智能"的基准，包含 520 个火灾世界、9074 个文本-图像交错问答对，覆盖物理状态理解、时序动力学、因果机制与干预推理四个能力维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实物理世界涉及多个连续物理场、潜在因果机制、部分观测与对干预敏感的动态过程，远超物体识别、场景描述和短期视觉预测的能力范围，因此需要新的评估手段来检验模型是否能从多模态部分观测中推断潜在状态、理解机制并预测干预后果。

Method: 以火灾作为规范化的压力测试环境（多物理场相互作用共同决定可观测状态与时间演化），构建 FireWorldBench。基准沿两个互补维度组织：物理能力轴（物理状态理解、时序动力学、因果机制、干预推理）与火灾场景任务轴。包含 520 个火灾世界条目（494 个受控仿真世界 + 26 个对齐真实世界的事件组），跨越 7 个环境族、47 种场景原型；数据融合结构化文本观测、2D 物理场可视化和 3D 事件级场景建模，形成 9074 个文本-图像交错问答对（选择题 + 开放式报告生成格式）。

Result: 构建了 FireWorldBench 基准，共 520 个火灾世界、9074 个文本-图像交错问答对，覆盖 7 个环境族、47 种场景原型，能够评估模型推断潜在物理状态、解释底层机制、预测耦合场演化及评估干预后果的能力。

Conclusion: FireWorldBench 为复杂物理世界智能提供了一个具有挑战性的测试平台，用于系统检验多模态大语言模型在耦合场、部分观测和干预敏感动态下的推理能力。

Abstract: Understanding the physical world requires more than object recognition, scene description, and short-term visual prediction, as real-world physical systems involve multiple continuous fields, latent causal mechanisms, partial observations, and intervention-sensitive dynamics. We propose FireWorldBench, a benchmark for evaluating complex physical world intelligence in multimodal large language models and agents through coupled-field fire dynamics. Fire provides a canonical stress-test environment, where multiple interacting physical fields jointly shape observable states and temporal dynamics. FireWorldBench is organized along two complementary axes, a physical capability axis and a fire scenario task axis, jointly covering physical-state understanding, temporal dynamics, causal mechanisms, and intervention reasoning. The benchmark comprises 520 fire-world entries, including 494 controlled simulation worlds and 26 real-world-aligned event groups, spanning 47 scene archetypes across 7 environment families. These entries combine structured textual observations, multiple 2D physical-field visualizations, and 3D event-level scene modeling, yielding 9,074 text-image interleaved question-answer pairs across choice-based and open-ended report-generation formats. FireWorldBench evaluates whether models can infer latent physical states, explain underlying mechanisms, forecast coupled-field evolution, and assess intervention consequences from multimodal partial observations, providing a challenging testbed for complex physical world intelligence.

</details>


### [139] [Tutoring Large Language Models to be Domain-adaptive, Precise and Safe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23071)
*Somnath Banerjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文提出“负责任智能”框架，从领域知识可靠性、实时伦理约束、文化多语言安全三方面系统提升AI的安全性与适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI在安全、伦理和文化敏感性方面存在关键挑战，包括专业领域知识幻觉、有害内容生成以及忽视语言文化规范等问题，亟需一个综合性的“负责任智能”框架。

Method: 论文推进三大核心方向：1）利用主动学习与基于图的知识提升专业领域适配，减少幻觉；2）提出解码时对齐机制，在生成过程中实时主动阻断有害文本；3）采用语言特定的引导（steering），尊重不同语言和社会规范以保障文化与多语言安全。

Result: 实现了一个集领域知识可靠、伦理合规和文化可适应于一体的框架，为构建情境知识丰富、伦理健全、文化可适应的下一代AI提供蓝图。

Conclusion: 通过将领域适配、伦理实时约束与文化多语言安全相结合，该工作为设计负责任、可信赖且文化敏感的下一代人工智能系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: This thesis proposes a framework for "responsible intelligence" to address AI's critical challenges in safety, ethics, and cultural sensitivity. It advances three core areas: First, it improves domain adaptation in specialized fields using active learning and graph-based knowledge to reduce hallucinations. Second, it enhances ethical rigor via a novel decoding-time alignment mechanism that proactively blocks harmful text generation in real-time. Finally, it ensures cultural and multilingual safety through language-specific steering that respects diverse linguistic and social norms. Ultimately, this work provides a blueprint for building next-generation AI that is contextually knowledgeable, ethically sound, and culturally adaptable.

</details>


### [140] [Event Signature Transfer: Model-Agnostic Forecast Scenario Construction from Historical Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23074)
*Karthik Sridhar,Aaditya Jain,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出事件签名迁移（EST）：一种无需训练、与模型无关的算子，可将已完成的过去事件转化为显式预测情景，通过剔除源事件的趋势与季节性并缩放重定时其"事件签名"，施加到原生预测上。EST 仅读取输出分位数，适用于任何分位数预测器，在多个模型与真实/合成场景中显著降低分位数损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测者常先知事件即将发生，却不知其影响的具体形态、规模与时机。现有方法（协变量条件化、激活编辑、原始回放）要么需要模型内部访问，要么需重新训练，难以灵活地将一个已观察到的相似事件作为情景施加到任意分位数预测器上。作者希望提供一种轻量、通用的事后情景构建方式。

Method: 引入 Event Signature Transfer（EST）算子：1）从源事件中剔除其自身趋势与季节性，分离出"事件签名"；2）对该签名进行缩放与重定时；3）将其迁移到原生预测上，同时保留原生预测的关联（链接）结构；4）强度为零时精确退化为原生预测。EST 仅读取输出分位数，训练无关、模型无关，无需访问模型内部。

Result: 在 12 个真实事件与 10 个合成场景上，对 Chronos-2、TimesFM-2.5、Toto-2.0 进行测试：人工配置的 EST 将真实事件的样本内 WQL 降低 21.7%–90%。在 Chronos-2 上，EST 在 12 项与协变量条件化、激活编辑、原始回放的对齐比较中胜出 11 项。

Conclusion: EST 提供了一种通用、免训练、仅依赖分位数输出的事件情景构建算子，能稳定提升分位数预测质量。作者明确指出：该算子构建的是"情景"，而非对该情景发生概率的估计。

Abstract: Forecasters often know an event is imminent but not the shape, size, or timing of its effect. We introduce Event Signature Transfer (EST), a training-free, model-agnostic operator that turns a completed past event into an explicit forecast scenario. EST removes a source event's own trend and seasonality, then scales and retimes the remaining event signature onto a native forecast, preserving the forecast's linked structure and reducing to it exactly at zero strength. Because it reads only output quantiles, EST applies to any quantile forecaster, with no training, no model internals, at transfer time. Across twelve real episodes and ten synthetic scenarios on Chronos-2, TimesFM-2.5 and Toto-2.0, manually configured EST reduces real-episode WQL by 21.7-90\% in-sample. On Chronos-2, it leads eleven of twelve matched comparisons against covariate conditioning, activation editing and raw replay. The operator builds a scenario; it does not estimate its likelihood.

</details>


### [141] [From Inference Engine to Inference Control Plane: Connecting vLLM, llm-d, and the Evolution of Efficient Distributed LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23130)
*Twinkll Sisodia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LLM 推理正从本地引擎优化演变为分布式控制问题，将 vLLM 视为执行引擎层、llm-d 视为推理控制平面层，并以综述形式综合既有研究，提出推理执行规划器（Inference Execution Planner）及配套的基准图谱、瓶颈迁移分类法、部署指南和基于 SLO-goodput 的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理的优化重点正在从单纯的引擎本地问题转向涉及可复用状态、阶段放置、异构加速器、网络、自动扩缩容、可靠性与服务水平目标（SLO）的分布式控制问题；现有工作分散于学术系统研究、开源实现与生产实践，缺乏统一视角来理解这一转型，且稀缺资源正从原始 FLOPs 转向受管状态、放置、网络移动、可靠性和决策质量。

Method: 本文采用综述/综合（synthesis）方法而非新基准：连接同行评审系统研究、开源实现和已记录的生产研究，将 vLLM（模型服务引擎，通过 PagedAttention、连续批处理、内核、量化、并行等机制优化执行）与 llm-d（推理控制平面，优化跨集群的何处、何时、以何种策略执行）视为互补层次。在此基础上提出推断执行规划器，选择可行执行计划（聚合 vs 分离拓扑、KV 来源与传输动作、硬件变体、路由/准入策略、慢尺度扩缩容决策），并给出源本地基准图谱、瓶颈迁移分类法、实用部署指南、基于 SLO-goodput 的评估框架，以及面向智能体、多模态、异构与弹性推理的研究问题。

Result: 综合证据表明现代推理中的稀缺资源正从单纯 FLOPs 转向受管状态、放置、网络移动、可靠性与决策质量；论文提出的推理执行规划器、基准图谱、瓶颈迁移分类法与 SLO-goodput 评估框架为理解与治理分布式推理提供了结构化工具。所有性能与部署结果均归因于原始来源（本文不新增基准数据）。

Conclusion: 应将 LLM 推理理解为一个跨引擎与控制平面的分布式控制问题；vLLM 与 llm-d 等层次互补，联合优化执行机制与编排策略。未来需关注智能体、多模态、异构与弹性推理等方向，并以受管状态、放置与决策质量作为核心优化对象。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference is evolving from an engine-local optimization problem into a distributed control problem involving reusable state, phase placement, heterogeneous accelerators, networking, autoscaling, reliability, and service-level objectives. This paper connects that transition across peer-reviewed systems research, open-source implementations, and documented production studies. It treats vLLM and llm-d as complementary layers: model-serving engines optimize execution through mechanisms such as PagedAttention, continuous batching, kernels, quantization, and parallelism, while an inference control plane can optimize where, when, and under what policy execution occurs across a fleet. The contribution is synthesis rather than a new benchmark; all reported performance and deployment results remain attributed to their original sources. The combined evidence suggests that the scarce resource in modern inference is shifting from raw FLOPs alone toward managed state, placement, network movement, reliability, and decision quality. We propose an Inference Execution Planner that selects feasible execution plans rather than only endpoints, including aggregated versus disaggregated topology, KV source and transfer action, hardware variant, routing/admission policy, and slower scaling decisions. We also provide a source-local benchmark atlas, a bottleneck-migration taxonomy, practical deployment guidance, an evaluation framework based on SLO-goodput, and research questions for agentic, multimodal, heterogeneous, and resilient inference.

</details>


### [142] [CraftBench-UE: Deterministic Evaluation for Coding Agents in Unreal Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23142)
*Shutong Wu,Kevin Calderone,Andy Tsen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍 CraftBenchUE，一个在隔离的 Unreal Engine 环境中评估智能体的测试框架，通过确定性构建、资源和运行时检查（无 LLM 评判）来验证游戏玩法实现，并基于 70 个任务评估了七个模型，发现 C++ 任务完成率显著高于 Blueprint，且通过资源检查的 Blueprint 提交有近半数未通过运行时断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在游戏引擎中实现玩法功能，仅仅代码能编译运行是远远不够的——能编译的代码未必实现了所要求的玩法。现有评估缺乏对游戏玩法正确性的确定性验证手段，因此需要一套不依赖 LLM 评判、可自动执行构建、资源与运行时检查的评估框架，以准确衡量智能体在真实游戏引擎环境中的玩法实现能力。

Method: 作者提出 CraftBenchUE 评估框架：在隔离的 Unreal Engine 环境中运行智能体，将智能体保存的提交内容重建到全新的项目中，并施加确定性的构建检查、资源（asset）检查与运行时检查，全程不使用 LLM 作为评判者。基于该框架构建了包含 70 个任务的基准，覆盖 C++ 源码、Blueprint 资源和编辑器脚本三类任务。实验在两种编辑器工具配置下评估七个模型，并设置了 C++ 任务的"文件+shell"基线；同时设计了 10 对配对任务，它们共享相同的玩法规格和运行时测试，但分别要求以 C++ 和 Blueprint 作为交付物。

Result: 在 10 个配对任务中，C++ 的完成率在两种工具配置下分别高出 Blueprint 30.0 和 42.9 个百分点。在配对任务中按时提交且通过资源检查的 Blueprint 提交里，分别有 42.2% 和 50.0% 未通过显式运行时断言——这些提交满足资源要求，却未能通过玩法测试。

Conclusion: 代码可编译不等于玩法被正确实现；Blueprint 任务相比 C++ 任务完成率明显更低，且大量通过资源检查的 Blueprint 提交实际未通过运行时玩法断言，揭示出"资源满足要求但玩法实现失败"这一容易被忽视的评估盲区。作者将发布该评估框架、任务基准及轨迹分析结果。

Abstract: Building gameplay features in a game engine requires more than code, as code that compiles and runs does not necessarily implement the requested gameplay. We introduce CraftBenchUE, an evaluation harness that runs agents in an isolated Unreal Engine environment, reconstructs their saved submissions in fresh projects, and applies deterministic build, asset, and runtime checks without an LLM judge. Based on the harness, we built a benchmark consisting of 70 tasks spanning C++ source, Blueprint assets, and editor scripting. We evaluate seven models under two editor-tool configurations, with a file-and-shell baseline on C++ tasks. We further pair tasks that specify the same gameplay and use the same runtime tests, but require C++ and Blueprint as the deliverables. Across the 10 paired tasks, C++ completion rates exceed Blueprint by 30.0 and 42.9 percentage points in the two tool configurations. Among on-time Blueprint submissions in this paired set that pass asset checks, 42.2% and 50.0% fail explicit runtime assertions. These submissions satisfy asset requirements but fail the required gameplay tests. We will release the harness, task benchmark, and our trajectory findings with the report.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Edge-centric Brain Transformer: An Edge-centric Functional Connectivity Learning Framework for fMRI-based Brain Disorder Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23782)
*Dengyi Zhao,Zhiheng Zhou,Mengyao Zhou,Yunping Wang,Xingqin Qi*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种边中心脑Transformer（EBT）框架，将静息态功能磁共振成像（rs-fMRI）分析重新表述为功能连接表示学习，以功能连接而非脑区为基本学习单元，并在多个数据集上取得超越现有方法的性能，同时发现可解释的疾病相关连接与模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法主要依赖节点中心表示（以脑区为学习单元），可能忽略蕴含在功能连接中的判别性病理改变，因此需要从连接视角挖掘疾病相关的连接模式。

Method: 提出EBT框架：构建边时间序列表示以捕捉功能连接的动态协同波动模式，将判别性连接组织成线图（line graph）进行显式的连接间建模；设计结构感知Transformer学习解剖相关连接的局部依赖与分布式功能网络间的全局交互；引入边级正交聚类读出模块，生成被试级表示并识别与脑疾病相关的潜在连接模块。

Result: 在多个神经影像数据集上，EBT一致优于代表性图神经网络、脑Transformer和传统基于连接的方法；可解释性分析揭示了稳定的疾病相关功能连接与连接模块，与已知病理网络改变一致。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了基于rs-fMRI脑疾病诊断的边中心视角，为发现可解释的连接生物标志物提供了有前景的框架，源代码已公开。

Abstract: Resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (rs-fMRI) enables the characterization of functional interactions among distributed brain regions and has shown promise for brain disorder diagnosis. However, existing deep learning methods predominantly rely on node-centric representations, where brain regions serve as the primary learning units, potentially overlooking discriminative alterations embedded in functional connections. Here, we propose an edge-centric brain transformer (EBT) framework that reformulates rs-fMRI analysis as functional connection representation learning. Instead of modeling brain regions independently, EBT constructs edge time-series representations to capture dynamic co-fluctuation patterns of functional connections and organizes discriminative connections into a line graph for explicit connection-to-connection modeling. A structure-aware transformer is developed to learn both local dependencies among anatomically related connections and global interactions across distributed functional networks. Furthermore, an edge-level orthogonal clustering readout module is introduced to derive subject-level representations and identify latent connectivity modules associated with brain disorders. Evaluations on multiple neuroimaging datasets demonstrate that EBT consistently outperforms representative graph neural networks, brain transformers, and conventional connectivity-based approaches. Interpretability analyses further reveal stable disease-associated functional connections and connectivity modules that align with known pathological network alterations. These findings establish an edge-centric perspective for rs-fMRI-based brain disorder diagnosis and provide a promising framework for discovering interpretable connectivity biomarkers. The source code is publicly available at: https://github.com/Zdy12/Edge-centric-Brain-Transformer.

</details>


### [144] [MoSAT: Human Motion Generation from Spatial Audio and Textual Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23797)
*Shuyang Xu,Zhiyang Dou,Yiduo Hao,Zekun Li,Liang Pan,Jingbo Wang,Cheng Lin,Yuan Liu,Wenping Wang,Mingmin Zhao,Taku Komura*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文研究空间音频与文本联合条件的人类动作合成任务，提出配对数据集STAM、基于潜在流匹配的生成框架MoSAT以及三模态评估器，在该任务上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类动作同时受外部声学事件（空间音频提供环境线索以引导动作响应）和行为意图（文本指定动作及执行方式）的共同塑造；然而此前的动作合成研究大多忽略了空间音频与文本的联合条件建模这一方向。

Method: 构建STAM数据集，将动作序列与空间音频和细粒度文本标注配对；提出MoSAT框架，在生成动作前通过分层交叉注意力将自然语言意图与方向性空间音频线索进行联合条件注入，采用潜在流匹配（latent flow-matching）实现全身动作生成，以增强时序一致性和语义对齐性；此外开发三模态评估器进行综合评测。

Result: 大量实验表明，MoSAT利用空间音频固有的动作塑造特性与文本语义相结合，在精确性和多样性上均取得SOTA性能，可在多种场景下生成精准多样的动作。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统研究空间音频与语言联合驱动的动作合成，STAM数据集填补了数据空白，MoSAT的分层交叉注意力设计有效融合双模态条件，为该新任务确立了基准与领先方案。

Abstract: Human motion is shaped by both external acoustic events and behavioral intent: spatial audio conveys environmental cues that elicit or guide a response, while text specifies the desired action and how it should be performed. In this paper, we study the novel task of human motion synthesis jointly conditioned on spatial audio and natural language, a problem that has been largely overlooked in previous research. To support this task, We introduce STAM, a dataset of motion sequences paired with spatial audio and detailed textual annotations whose rich vocabulary affords precise and nuanced specification of human motions. We further introduce MoSAT, a latent flow-matching framework for full-body motion generation jointly conditioned on natural-language intent and directional spatial-audio cues through hierarchical cross-attention before generating motion. Such a hierarchical design enhances temporally coherent and semantically aligned motion sequences. We also develop tri-modal evaluators for comprehensive evaluation on this novel task. Extensive experiments show that MoSAT achieves the SOTA performance by leveraging spatial audio's intrinsic motion-shaping properties alongside textual semantics, enabling precise and diverse motion in various scenarios.

</details>


### [145] [Constrained Program Generation for 3D Reaction Animation with a 0.8B Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24457)
*Hongyuan Wang,Daming Luo,Nico Pietroni,Christy Liang,Andrew McDonagh*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: ChemXRG 是一种面向化学反应动画的领域专用语言（DSL）框架，将反应动画表示为可执行程序，通过持久原子标识符与显式键/电荷/基团操作连接符号反应与三维变换，并用反应约束与执行检查保障一致性，最终由 0.8B 模型 ChemQwen 生成动画程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手段（化学方程式、结构式、分子查看器）对化学反应只提供互补的静态描述，要组装可交互的三维解释既需人工指定分子变化，又要逐一核对一致性，缺乏统一的表示来桥接符号化学与可视化。

Method: 提出 ChemXRG 这一 DSL 框架：将反应动画编码为可执行程序；用持久原子标识符和显式的键、电荷、基团操作将符号反应与可视变换关联；基于已知的原子映射反应物/产物结构，用"反应接地约束"固定输入决定的事实并限制动作选择；在执行时验证变换；训练 0.8B 的 ChemQwen 模型生成 DSL 代码，并构建反应程序语料库。

Result: 成对与组件评估显示，在输入条件约束下，编译器接受率和归一化全程序一致率均有提升；同时识别出仍需执行检查来捕获的残余失败；公开浏览器应用演示了从符号反应描述到可审查程序再到交互式 3D 动画的完整链路。

Conclusion: ChemXRG 通过统一的共享表示使生成、验证与渲染作用于同一套变化描述，有效弥合了符号化学与三维可视化的鸿沟；未来工作将针对执行检查暴露的剩余失败进一步优化。

Abstract: Visualizing a chemical reaction requires making its molecular changes visible while keeping the animation faithful to the stated chemistry. Equations, structural diagrams and molecular viewers provide complementary descriptions, but assembling an interactive three-dimensional explanation still requires specifying the changes and checking their consistency. We present ChemXRG, a domain-specific language (DSL) framework that addresses this gap by representing a reaction animation as an executable program. Persistent atom identifiers and explicit bond, charge and grouping operations connect the symbolic reaction to the displayed transformation. This shared representation lets generation, verification and rendering operate on the same account of what changes. Given known, atom-mapped reactant and product structures, reaction-grounded constraints fix input-determined facts and restrict action choices; execution checks validate the resulting transformation before geometry and frames are constructed. We implement this paradigm with a reaction-program corpus and ChemQwen, a trained 0.8B DSL generator. Paired and component evaluations show improved compiler acceptance and normalized full-program agreement under input-conditioned constraints, while identifying remaining failures that require execution checks. A public browser application demonstrates the connection from symbolic reaction descriptions to inspectable programs and interactive 3D animations.

</details>
